Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

CTCP Dịch vụ Hàng không Taseco (AST) 
Tiếp tục mở rộng điểm kinh doanh 
22/09/2026  
Chuyên viên cao cấp Nguyễn Ngọc Anh 
(+84) 24-7303-5333 anhntn@kbsec.com.vn

KQKD tăng trưởng tích cực trong 
2Q2026

Tăng trưởng khách qua cảng hàng 
không thúc đẩy doanh thu tại các 
điểm kinh doanh hiện hữu

Mở rộng mạng lưới tạo động lực tăng 
trưởng doanh thu mới

Cơ cấu kinh doanh và hiệu quả vận 
hành hỗ trợ cải thiện biên lợi nhuận

Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA, giá mục 
tiêu 76,500 VND/cổ phiếu

2Q2026, AST ghi nhận doanh thu và LNST đạt 479/92 tỷ VND (+22%/19% yoy), với 
động lực chủ yếu đến từ (1) đóng góp từ 19 điểm kinh doanh mở mới tại T3 Tân Sơn 
Nhất và (2) tăng trưởng tích cực của các mảng hàng miễn thuế, nhà hàng - fastfood 
và phòng chờ thương gia. Lũy kế 6T2026, doanh thu và LNST đạt 1,035/221 tỷ VND 
(+33%/46% yoy), lần lượt hoàn thành 48%/49% kế hoạch năm.

Chúng tôi dự phóng lượng khách quốc tế/nội địa tại các thị trường AST khai thác 
tăng bình quân 11%/5% mỗi năm đến 2030. Lưu lượng và tỷ trọng khách quốc tế 
gia tăng được kỳ vọng thúc đẩy số giao dịch và doanh thu bình quân trên mỗi 
khách, đặc biệt tại mảng miễn thuế, bán lẻ và phòng chờ.

Chúng tôi dự phóng số điểm kinh doanh AST tăng từ 145 điểm cuối năm 2026 lên 
182 điểm năm 2030. Sau đóng góp từ 19 điểm tại T3 Tân Sơn Nhất và 6 điểm mới tại 
T2 Nội Bài từ 2025 - 2026, các dự án Long Thành, Phú Quốc và Gia Bình tiếp tục mở 
ra dư địa mở rộng mạng lưới trong trung và dài hạn cho doanh nghiệp.

Việc mở rộng các hoạt động mang lại lợi nhuận cao, bao gồm hàng miễn thuế, 
phòng chờ thương gia và tối ưu hoạt động tại các điểm kinh doanh được kỳ vọng 
tác động tích cực lên biên lợi nhuận của AST. Chúng tôi dự phóng biên lợi nhuận 
gộp đạt 64.7%/65.5% trong năm 2026/2027, hỗ trợ LNST công ty mẹ tăng lần lượt 
48%/17% yoy.

Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA đối với AST, giá mục tiêu 76,500 VND/cổ phiếu, tương 
đương tiềm năng tăng giá 20% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 21/09/2026.

Cơ cấu doanh thu 2025 Hoạt động kinh doanh

CTCP Dịch vụ Hàng không Taseco là một 
trong những doanh nghiệp có quy mô mạng 
lưới điểm kinh doanh lớn trong lĩnh vực dịch 
vụ phi hàng không tại Việt Nam, với 141 
điểm kinh doanh tại 6 sân bay quốc tế tính 
đến cuối tháng 6/2026. Hoạt động cốt lõi của 
công ty bao gồm bán lẻ bách hóa lưu niệm 
(BHLN), hàng miễn thuế, dịch vụ ăn uống và 
phòng chờ thương gia. Ngoài ra, AST còn 
tham gia lĩnh vực suất ăn hàng không thông 
qua khoản đầu tư tại công ty liên kết VinaCS. 
Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Điểm nhấn đầu tư  Chú thích 
Doanh thu trên các điểm kinh doanh hiện hữu tăng nhờ lượng khách quốc tế/nội địa dự phóng tăng 
11%/5% mỗi năm đến 2030. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Mở rộng mạng lưới tạo động lực tăng trưởng mới, với số điểm kinh doanh AST 2026 dự kiến đạt 145 
điểm và tăng lên 182 điểm vào 2030. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Biên lợi nhuận gộp cải thiện lên 64.7%/65.5% trong năm 2026/2027 nhờ mở rộng hàng miễn thuế, 
phòng chờ và tối ưu vận hành. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo

(tỷ VNĐ) Q2/2025 Q2/2026 +/-%YoY 1H2025 1H2026 +/-%YoY Chú thích 
Doanh thu 394 479 22% 779 1,035 33% Hoàn thành 48.1% doanh thu kế hoạch 2026 (2,150

Biểu đồ 5. Doanh thu/điểm kinh doanh của AST

Nguồn:AST

Tăng trưởng khách qua cảng hàng 
không thúc đẩy doanh thu tại các 
điểm kinh doanh hiện hữu 
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng tăng trưởng lưu lượng hành khách, đặc biệt là sự gia tăng tỷ 
trọng khách quốc tế, sẽ thúc đẩy doanh thu và hiệu quả kinh doanh của AST 
trong giai đoạn 2026–2030. Theo Cục Hàng không Việt Nam, lượng khách thông 
qua các cảng hàng không trong 7T2026 đạt hơn 76 triệu lượt (+7% yoy), trong 
đó khách quốc tế đạt gần 30 triệu lượt (+11% yoy), trong khi khách nội địa ghi 
nhận tăng trưởng 4% yoy. Xu hướng này được hỗ trợ bởi nhu cầu du lịch quốc 
tế, chính sách thị thực thuận lợi và việc mở rộng mạng bay, trong khi tăng 
trưởng kinh tế và thu nhập tiếp tục tạo nền tảng cho nhu cầu đi lại trong nước. 
Trên cơ sở đó, chúng tôi dự phóng lượt khách quốc tế và nội địa tại các thị 
trường AST khai thác tăng bình quân lần lượt 11% và 5%/năm đến 2030, qua đó 
nâng dần tỷ trọng khách quốc tế trong tổng lưu lượng, thúc đẩy doanh 
thu/điểm kinh doanh nhờ: (1) lưu lượng hành khách qua cảng hàng không gia 
tăng và (2) tỷ trọng khách quốc tế cao hơn hỗ trợ doanh thu bình quân trên mỗi 
khách, đặc biệt tại mảng miễn thuế, bán lẻ và phòng chờ.

 Biểu đồ 7. Doanh thu/điểm kinh doanh AST (tỷ VND)

Nguồn: AST  Nguồn: AST

Mở rộng mạng lưới tại các nhà ga 
hiện hữu và sân bay mới, gia tăng 
quy mô kinh doanh đến 2030 
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng chu kỳ mở rộng hạ tầng hàng không giai đoạn 2026–2030 sẽ 
tạo dư địa gia tăng quy mô kinh doanh cho AST, với số điểm kinh doanh dự 
phóng đạt 145 điểm vào cuối 2026 và tăng lên 182 điểm vào năm 2030. Cụ thể: 
- Trong ngắn hạn, tăng trưởng năm 2026 được hỗ trợ bởi việc ghi nhận 
đủ năm doanh thu từ 19 điểm kinh doanh mở mới tại T3 Tân Sơn Nhất 
trong năm 2025, cùng 6 điểm mới tại T2 Nội Bài trong năm 2026, trong 
đó có 2 cửa hàng miễn thuế và 2 cửa hàng Memories of Vietnam khai 
trương trong 2Q2026. 
- Trong trung và dài hạn, các dự án Cảng HKQT Long Thành, Cảng HKQT 
Phú Quốc nâng cấp mở rộng và Cảng HKQT Gia Bình sẽ góp phần thúc 
đẩy ngành hàng không Việt Nam nói chung, bổ sung mặt bằng thương 
mại, mở ra cơ hội phát triển các mảng kinh doanh cốt lõi cho AST nói 
riêng bao gồm các mảng bán lẻ, miễn thuế, nhà hàng - fastfood và 
phòng chờ thương gia. Với kinh nghiệm khai thác 141 điểm kinh doanh 
tại 6 sân bay quốc tế và lịch sử trúng thầu tại T3 Tân Sơn Nhất, T2 Nội 
Bài trong thời gian qua, chúng tôi kỳ vọng AST có khả năng cạnh tranh 
cao trong việc đấu thầu quyền khai thác mặt bằng tại các sân bay mới, 
đặc biệt là Gia Bình, qua đó duy trì vị thế và thị phần kinh doanh phi 
hàng không tại khu vực phía Bắc khi một phần lưu lượng hành khách 
được phân bổ lại từ Nội Bài.

Cơ cấu kinh doanh và hiệu quả vận 
hành hỗ trợ mở rộng biên lợi nhuận  
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng biên lợi nhuận của AST tiếp tục cải thiện trong 2026 - 2027, 
nhờ định hướng mở rộng các mảng có biên lợi nhuận tốt như hàng miễn thuế 
và phòng chờ thương gia, kết hợp tối ưu hiệu quả vận hành tại các điểm kinh 
doanh. Cụ thể: (1) AST ưu tiên gia tăng số điểm kinh doanh hàng miễn thuế 
nhằm đón đầu tăng trưởng nhanh và ổn định của lượng khách quốc tế, qua đó 
nâng đóng góp lợi nhuận từ hoạt động mua sắm tại sân bay, (2) mở rộng mạng 
lưới phòng chờ, đồng thời gia tăng lượng khách sử dụng trên nền năng lực phục 
vụ hiện hữu và (3) tối ưu hóa hiệu quả hoạt động thông qua cải thiện bố trí mặt 
bằng, danh mục sản phẩm và kiểm soát chi phí tại các điểm kinh doanh, qua 
đó tăng doanh thu và lợi nhuận thu được trên mỗi khách hàng.

Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA, giá 
mục tiêu 76,500 VND/cổ phiếu 
 Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA đối với cổ phiếu AST, với giá mục tiêu 76,500 
VND/cổ phiếu, tương đương tiềm năng tăng giá 20% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 
21/09/2026, dựa trên triển vọng tăng trưởng từ lượng khách quốc tế, mở rộng 
mạng lưới điểm kinh doanh và khả năng cải thiện biên lợi nhuận của doanh 
nghiệp. Chúng tôi sử dụng phương pháp định giá từng phần (SOTP), bao gồm 
DCF cho hoạt động kinh doanh cốt lõi của AST và P/E mục tiêu 11x đối với phần 
lợi nhuận thuộc sở hữu AST tại VinaCS. Các giả định chính gồm: (1) lượng khách 
quốc tế/nội địa tăng trung bình 11%/5% mỗi năm đến 2030, (2) số điểm kinh 
doanh tăng từ 145 điểm cuối 2026 lên 182 điểm năm 2030, với giả định AST 
trúng thầu mặt bằng tại cả Long Thành và Gia Bình và (3) biên lợi nhuận gộp 
2026/2027 đạt 64.7/65.5%. Đối với VinaCS, chúng tôi kỳ vọng nhà máy suất ăn 
Long Thành sẽ hỗ trợ tăng trưởng lợi nhuận trong trung và dài hạn khi dự kiến 
có thể bắt đầu đóng góp lợi nhuận cho AST từ 2028.

Rủi ro đối với định giá bao gồm: (1) AST không giành được quyền khai thác tại 
Long Thành và Gia Bình như kỳ vọng, (2) lưu lượng khách dịch chuyển giữa các 
sân bay mới và hiện hữu khiến doanh thu tại một số điểm kinh doanh bị sụt 
giảm, (3) cạnh tranh gia tăng từ các doanh nghiệp kinh doanh dịch vụ phi hàng 
không khác và (4) căng thẳng địa chính trị kéo dài, giá dầu tăng gây áp lực lên 
hoạt động hàng không và chi tiêu của hành khách.

## Document 227: EcoNews of 12 October 2026 (2026-10-12)

Source PDF: all report pdf/EcoNews of 12 October 2026 (2026-10-12).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-12
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

The bank
for a changing
world
5
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.37 / 12 October 2026ECONEWS
Find out more in our scenario and forecasts
ADVANCED ECONOMIES
EUROPEAN UNION
Stronger trade posture pays off. The EU and China have agreed to
restrain hybrid cars exports to the EU to a level half or less than cur -
rently projected over the next four years, cutting exports by “several
million units” though still allowing them to rise. China also agreed to

drop its tariffs on EUR 4 bn of EU exports and provide easier access to
Chinese rare earths and magnets under a fast-track licensing sche -
me. These announcements were made at the conclusion of EU Trade
Commissioner Maroš Šefcovic’s visit to Beijing, following a recent es -
calation in trade tensions. Meanwhile, France and Germany released
a joint non-paper on Strengthening European resilience, addressing
global macroeconomic imbalances and market distorting practices

calling notably for:
i) Broadening the tools the European Commission may use to address
unfair trade practices (including undervalued exchange rates) or im -
port surges;
ii) Procedural changes to make them easier to use (qualified majority
of member states needed to block rather than authorise them). These
measures will be discussed during the next EU council on October
15-16th.
The European Commission also unveiled a new critical materials pac -

kage involving 46 strategic extraction, processing or recycling projects
spread across 16 member states (including 5 in France). The goal is to
reduce the EU’s dependence on China for critical metals.
EUROZONE
Price pressures are broadening, activity holds up. Producer price in -
flation accelerated further in August (8.3% y/y 1 from 5.8%), driven by
energy. Pressures are becoming more widespread, notably in capital
goods and above all intermediate goods (6.8%). They are partly offset

by non-durable consumer goods, which remain in deflation (-0.8%).
Given the PPI's close correlation with the HICP , this points to further
upside risks to consumer inflation. On the activity front, final September
PMIs confirmed the flash estimates, with the composite index still solid
at 53.1 and the manufacturing index stable at 52.7. The construction
PMI improved but remains depressed (43.4; +0.4 pt). Retail sales edged
up 0.1% m/m2 in August.

France
Modest spread relief as budget positioning starts. The spread between
French and German 10Y yields narrowed to 136 bps 3 on October 9
(mostly thanks to a decline in the French yield). As the Finance Com -
mittee of the National Assembly started discussing the 2027 budget,
the government reiterated its strong intent not to deviate from the
5% of GDP deficit target and to get a budget adopted in time, likely
without a vote. This is the most likely outcome — but not to be im -

plemented before 10 December (given mandatory timelines) — which
effectively removes any incentive for MPs to back unpopular measures
during the process. We can therefore expect heated debates (including
multiple no-confidence votes being tabled but rejected) and a lack in
fiscal discipline. In parallel, the "Rassemblement National" party ("Na-
tional Rally" in English) presented its fiscal strategy, which includes an
alternative budget for 2027 and an aggressive fiscal consolidation path

throughout 2032. The party called for a referendum to constitutionalise
1 Year-on-year
2 Month-on-month
3 Basis points
a “golden rule” that would require a deficit at least 0.5 pp below the
debt-stabilising threshold (roughly 3% of GDP), as long as the public
debt ratio exceeds 60% of GDP . The golden rule would require a budget
balance adjustment of 0.5 pp per year if the fiscal deficit is more than
1 pp above the stabilising threshold. The adjustment measures put

forward lack specifics but likely overstate savings, understate costs
and rely on overly optimistic growth assumptions (despite large fiscal
consolidation, at least 1.7% annually over the next three years — a
pace reached only 4 times in France since 2008, apart from post-pan-
demic reopening, and well in excess of the roughly 1% growth potential
estimated by most international institutions). That said, it is significant
that this party has chosen to fly the flag for fiscal discipline loud and

clear. Other parties should do the same.
Activity is holding up. New vehicle registrations rose 11% y/y in Sep -
tember, confirming a marked acceleration over the past three months
(+9.4% y/y in Q3). In Q3, growth came entirely from electric vehicles
(+112% y/y), whose share of registrations reached an all-time high
(38% in Q3, 41% in September). Export growth continued in August,
both in services (+5.2% y/y YTD) and in goods (+5.6% y/y YTD) — though

driven by prices rather than volume in the latter case. Manufacturing
output posted only a moderate rebound in August (+0.3% m/m; fol -
lowing cumulative declines of 2.8% over the previous three months),
limited by renewed supply constraints in Q3 (which limited produc -
tion at 31.5% of companies; +5 pp compared to H1). Industrial produc-
tion fell (-0.3% m/m) as a result of a decline in electricity production
(-2.4% m/m) driven by the heatwave. Credit growth remains dynamic.

Bank and market financing to NFCs saw steady growth in August (+4%).
New housing loans rebounded slightly in August (EUR 12.2 bn) fol -
lowing a relatively weak July, whilst interest rates continued to rise.
Consumer credit remained buoyant.
Upcoming: Banque de France monthly Business Survey (Monday), Bu-
siness creations in September (Friday).
Germany
Bullish official growth forecast upgrade despite mixed data. The
German government raised its 2026 GDP growth forecast to 1.3%

— the fastest pace since 2017 — from a prior estimate of 0.5%, citing
stronger-than-expected exports and a surge in government spending.
The 2027 forecast was also raised to 1.1% from 0.9%. Industrial pro -
duction was stronger than expected in August (+2.3% y/y versus -1.7%
in July; the highest level since March 2025 and higher than antici -
pated), driven mainly by strong growth in construction output, prima -
rily in public works (+10.5%; to the highest level since November 2021).

By contrast, manufacturing output fell by -0.2% (after +3.3% in July)
due to a decline in transport equipment production (lowest level since
April 2022). Likewise, new factory orders hit their lowest level in one
year in August (-10.6% m/m). However, this decline is mainly due
to a base effect. Export growth remains solid: +4.4% YTD, despite a
-0.8% m/m decline in August. Exports to the Eurozone continue to rise
(+6.3% y/y YTD), while exports to the U.S. have returned to positive

territory (+1.1%) and those to China continue to decline (-12.9%). The
trade surplus reached EUR 19.5 bn in August (+4% y/y YTD, despite a
-9.8% m/m decline).

### Page 2

6
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoWeek 26.37 / 12 October 2026

ECONEWS Find out more in our scenario and forecasts
UNITED STATES
Mild slowdown in services and more rate hikes signaled. The non-ma-
nufacturing ISM survey fell 0.5 pp4 to 54.9 in September due to a sharp
slowdown in the business activity component (down 5.2 pp to 56.5 pp,
still a robust expansion). Employment rose and supply pressures in -
tensified for the first time since June, while the price gauge reached

its highest level (74, up 1.4 pp) since 2022. Meanwhile, the minutes
of the September FOMC meeting confirmed the Fed’s hawkish outlook
and noted that the AI-buildout carried growing weight in the reading of
current inflation data and outlook. Governor Waller and St. Louis Fed
President Musalem mentioned the need for additional rate hikes while
remaining flexible on timing. The New York Fed survey of consumer in-
flation expectations showed them rising to 3.9%. We continue to expect

two more hikes, the next one coming in December.
Upcoming: September CPI (Wednesday), September PPI and Retail
Sales (Thursday), September Industrial Production (Friday).
JAPAN
Solid wage growth fails to lift household spending. Total wage growth
eased to 3.8% y/y in August (down 0.5 pp) as bonus payments stalled
(0% y/y, after 5.3% y/y in July). This allowed continued real wage growth
at 1.5% y/y, extending an uninterrupted run of positive readings in 2026

as administrative measures kept inflation in check. However, this did
not prevent real household spending from contracting in August for the
ninth consecutive month (-3.1% y/y).
EMERGING MARKETS
The IIF reports that nonresident portfolio flows to EMs turned negative
at -USD 26.3 bn in September, after USD 11.4 bn in August, the first net
outflow since June, as a hawkish Fed lifted US real yields and the USD.
Equity outflows reached -USD 19.2 bn, led by heavy foreign selling of

Korean equities, while debt flows turned negative at -USD 7.0 bn as
hard currency demand faded and EM credit spreads widened late in
the month. Further hikes from DM central banks will create additional
headwinds, but EM should remain resilient as long as global growth
persists.
ASIA
China
Yuan policy defence. Coinciding with the Beijing visit of the EU trade
commissioner, the People's Bank of China issued an unusually lengthy
formal statement rejecting claims that the yuan is undervalued and

defending its exchange-rate management by reiterating that it has a
‘floating exchange rate regime’ with reference to a basket of currencies.
It further argued that China's trade surpluses are driven by rising in -
dustrial competitiveness rather than currency under-devaluation and
that exchange-rate adjustments will not resolve structural global im -
balances. Nonetheless, it allowed the yuan to hit a four-year high vs.
the CFETS basket — bringing its year-to-date gain to 5.7%. The PBOC

set the USD/CNY's reference rate at 6.7330, the strongest level since
2023.
India
Monetary tightening. The RBI raised its repo rate by 25bp to 5.5%, its
first hike since February 2023, and shifted to a “calibrated tightening”
stance amidst rising inflationary pressures. The RBI introduced new
measures to stabilise the rupee, including direct dollar supplies to
state-owned oil companies and tighter restrictions on FX derivatives.
4 Percentage points

EUROPE, MIDDLE EAST, AFRICA
Central Europe
Cautious monetary policy stance amid mild inflation uptick. Poland’s
and Romania’s Central banks kept policy rates unchanged at 3.75% and
6.5%, respectively. Inflation accelerated in September, with a mild up-
tick in the Czech Republic and Hungary (respectively 2.5% and 1.6% y/y)
and a more entrenched increase in Poland (4% y/y). Inflation is now
above the official target in the Czech Rep. and Poland, but fuel price

caps in both countries should contain inflation dynamics.
Gulf
PMIs for September point for a firm but uneven non-oil recovery. Both
Saudi and UAE PMIs stood at 55.3, driven mostly by export orders in
the UAE and by domestic demand in Saudi Arabia. On the contrary,
Qatar’s PMI fell again (to 46.7), while Kuwait’s PMI also declined but
remained in expansionary zone (at 52.4).
Türkiye
Manufacturing production contracted by 1.6% m/m in August, the lar-

gest monthly decrease since the beginning of the year. On a y/y ba -
sis, it fell by 2.3%. Both domestic demand and exports seem to have
contributed negatively to this poor outcome. On a positive note, in
September, CPI inflation (1.9% m/m, 27.9% y/y) slowed more than the
market expected, and exports rebounded.
LATIN AMERICA
Inflationary pressures slightly up across the region. In Brazil, headline
CPI inflation accelerated to 4.6% y/y from 4.2% y/y in August (above the

central bank's 4.5% target), partly reflecting the reversal of a tempora-
ry electricity price discount in August. In Mexico, inflation rose to 3.5%
in September from 3.3% in August, while in Colombia it reached 6.3%,
slightly up from 6.2% in August. Inflation remained high in Chile but
was stable at 4.1%. In Peru, it was also slightly up (4.2% vs. 4.1%); the
central bank kept its policy rate unchanged at 4.25% for the thirteenth
consecutive month but maintained an upward bias amid rising inflation

and energy prices.
COMMODITIES
Energy: Limited relief. Continuation of skirmishes in the Gulf of Hormuz
and escalation of the conflict between the Houthis and a Saudi-led
coalition left oil prices fluctuating around $100/b for Brent. However,
the complex security situation has been driving the cost of shipping to
record high levels and the price of physical barrel to early April levels.
Late on Friday, President Trump announced an agreement with Russian

President Putin to release to global markets 4.8 million tonnes of die -
sel (roughly 8 days of European consumption), provided conditions in
refineries allow for it, of which 300,000 tonnes immediately. The White
House also announced a suspension of sanctions on Russia’s exports
of diesel until April 2027. Energy experts expect a de minimis price
impact.
European gas prices (TTF) rose by 11.5% over the week to 82 €/MWh.
EU gas inventories reached 73.6% of full capacities on October 11 th

(the official target is 80% at end October).
Food inflation rose sharply in September. The FAO’s Food Price Index
grew by 5.8% y/y, up from just 3% in August. This was driven by cereal
(+17% y/y) and sugar prices (+15%).

## Document 236: Energy dominates Polish CPI, while broader inflation remains contained

Source PDF: all report pdf/Energy dominates Polish CPI, while broader inflation remains contained.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-14
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Poland’s September inﬂation was revised up to 4.1% YoY, with
energy and fuel prices accounting for roughly 60% of the
annual increase. Meanwhile, core inﬂation even eased, and
there is still little evidence that the energy shock is passing
through more broadly into other prices. The central bank should
not rush to hike interest rates

Rafal Benecki and Adam Antoniak
A 9.2% monthly jump in fuel prices contributed to higher inﬂation in
September
Higher energy prices boosted inﬂation in
September
Poland’s StatOﬃce revised its estimate of September CPI inﬂation to
4.1% YoY from the 4.0% YoY reported previously. Goods price
inﬂation accelerated to 3.6% YoY and services prices slowed to
5.3% YoY, compared with 2.5% YoY and 5.6% YoY respectively in
August. Services inﬂation has fallen to its lowest level in ﬁve
months, with no evidence of second-round eﬀects.

### Page 2

Price growth continues to be driven primarily by rising energy costs.
Compared with August, fuel prices increased by 9.2% MoM, while
household energy prices rose by 1.0% MoM, mainly due to higher
prices for liquid fuels (+14.7% MoM) and solid fuels (+5.5% MoM),
including coal (+7.4% MoM). Higher energy prices contributed
around 2.5 percentage points, or roughly 60% of September's
annual inﬂation rate.
Food prices were broadly unchanged (+0.1% MoM), as were prices in

core categories. We estimate that core inﬂation excluding food and
energy slowed to around 3.1% YoY from 3.3% YoY in August. The
deceleration in services inﬂation suggests that the energy shock is
not spreading into other price categories. In September, even
categories linked to the AI investment boom were not very
buoyant.

### Page 3

Poland's (NBP) tolerance band around its inﬂation target. The main
reason is the reintroduction of government ﬁscal intervention,
which reduces excise duties and VAT rates on fuels during the ﬁnal
three months of this year.
In our view, if oil prices remain elevated, the ﬁscal programme is
likely to be extended in the ﬁrst two months of 2027 despite the
challenging ﬁscal situation. This would help avoid a sharp rise in CPI
at the beginning of 2027, when the termination of the programme

would otherwise push fuel prices higher. Suspending the scheme in
March 2027 would allow this eﬀect to coincide with a high base,
generated by the start of the war in 2026, and smooth headline
inﬂation.
An external supply shock with limited
broader price pressures
At present, higher inﬂation is primarily the result of an external
supply shock in the form of rising energy prices, while prices in
other categories remain relatively stable. Food remains cheap, and

there are no clear signs of broader inﬂationary pressure or
signiﬁcant second-round eﬀects.
However, the prolonged energy crisis has somewhat worsened the
inﬂation outlook. The beginning of next year will probably bring
substantial increases in regulated household prices, particularly
electricity and gas tariﬀs. Food prices are also likely to rise,
reﬂecting higher agricultural commodity prices, a low comparison
base and more expensive fertilisers.

The MPC can afford to wait, but rate hikes
in 1Q27 cannot be ruled out
We believe that, under current conditions, the Monetary Policy
Council does not need to rush into tightening monetary policy. The
November inﬂation projection may initiate a discussion about
whether interest rate increases are warranted.
In our view, rate hikes could take place in 1Q27, as further months
of elevated energy prices increase the risk that the energy shock

## Document 274: Indonesia_ Policy mix aligns (2026-10-12)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Indonesia_ Policy mix aligns (2026-10-12).pdf
Report source: DBS
Date: 2026-10-12
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

SHIFT IN NARRATIVE

After a rough first half of the year, the 
narrative around Indonesia’s economy has 
turned relatively constructive . The macro 
outlook is on the mend, and policy coherence 
has strengthened. Recent budget 
developments have reduced the likelihood of an 
outright fiscal slippage. A more disciplined 
fiscal trajectory should also provide room for 
monetary policy to stay focused on its price 
stability mandate rather than on ad dressing 
financial stability risks due to fiscal concerns.

On the markets front, e ven as external triggers 
impacted the rupiah, the one -sided sharp 
depreciation run in the currency in the first half 
of the year has stalled . Debt markets have  
attracted offshore interest, alongside strong 
demand for shorter-tenor SRBI securities, all of 
which have contributed to the overall capital 
flows picture.

Ahead of MSCI’s final take on the domestic 
equity market’s classification in November, 
however, equity markets  find little favour . 
Nonetheless, material improvement s in the 
stock listing s and regulatory requirements 
undertaken by the stock market and financial 
regulators are expected to contribute to the 
medium-term stability of the capital markets.

Key domestic risks that plagued the economy 
in the first half of the year have been alleviated 
to some extent, while a few other monitorables 
remain.

Indonesia: Policy mix aligns October 12, 2026

NEAR-TERM FISCAL WORRIES 
RECEDE

Fiscal fears have moderated in the near 
term. New Finance Minister Suahasil 
Nazara reaffirmed the government’s 
commitment to maintain the annual 
deficit below -3% of GDP threshold. The 
2026 run-rate suggests that the deficit is 
likely to close within the budgeted target.

By September 2026, the fiscal deficit 
stood at 1.2% of GDP , reflecting a 
normalisation in spending and firm 
revenue growth. Total spending rose by 
a still -strong 1 9% yoy , even though the 
pace moderated from 1H, backed by 
disbursements towards the flagship free 
food program, subsidy and 
compensation outlays , as well as 
personnel expenditure.  Realised 
expenditure stood at 69% of the full -
year budget by 9M26.

On the other hand, revenues rose sharply 
by 25 .5% yoy (partly benefiting from 
base effects as readings were negative 
at the same time last year), helped by 
higher tax collections, export duties, and 
non-tax receipts. Revenues have already 
accounted for 74% of the budgeted 
scale by 9M26, suggesting full -year 
target is likely to be met.

Global crude prices are a key risk for the 
fiscal outlook, as prevailing Brent prices 
are above $100bl at the time of writing 
(vs budgeted ICP at $70bl), which will 
feed through higher subsidy and 
compensation outgoes, as authorities 
remain hesitant to raise fuel pump prices.

Significant efficiency measures have, 
however, been put in place  to contain 
spending, including reductions in 
outlays towards flagship schemes , 
which will help contain the extent of 
deterioration in the fiscal math. For 
instance, the flagship MBG program, 
which started with an allocation of IDR 
35trn, was  moderated to IDR 268trn 
(realised spending by Sep26 was IDR 
153trn). Successive stimulus packages

### Page 3

Indonesia: Policy mix aligns October 12, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            3

have been announced but the scale has 
been contained.

2027 math signals fiscal prudence, soft 
spots warrant attention

The official goalposts for 2027 point to 
a continued preference for fiscal 
prudence rather than fiscal activism.

Spending is on course to surpass the 
symbolic IDR 4000tn level, ris ing nearly 
4% yoy, with outlays divided between 
the centre and regional governments. 
The mix points to steady allocations 
towards subsidies, social transfers , and 
capital expenditure,  with ministry -level 
spending (K/L) set to contract, while non-
K/L spending is budgeted to increase. 
More notably, spending is expected to 
rise on key flagship programs – for 
instance, social protection (up 10%), the 
largest component education (up 14%) , 
which includes the free meals program 
at IDR 240trn.

On the revenue end, state revenues are 
expected to rise by 9% yoy compared to 
the budgeted 2026 APBN/ 7% vs the 
revised 2026 outlook. Compared to 
baseline 2026, tax revenues are 
expected to rise 10.5%, besides higher 
non-tax collections. This math lands the 
2027 budgeted deficit at -2.4% of GDP 
vs revised 2026 at -2.85% of GDP.

Few uncertainties that loom.  Firstly, 
next year’s math rests on an upbeat 
growth projection of 6 .0% (DBSf 5.2%) , 
besides modest depreciation in the 
rupiah and about  a 7% increase in the 
Indonesia crude price assumption (see 
table). Some of these expectations 
might be challenged as the year unfolds. 
Next, state revenues are expected to 
rise to a n upbeat  12.5% of GDP , likely 
riding on a strong growth forecast, 
better tax due diligence , and improved 
administration via the Coretax 
mechanism.

Thirdly, crude prices are a key risk to the 
fiscal math. Given the economy’s 
position as a net oil importer, various 
official commentaries put the sensitivity  
at every $1pb oil worsening the fiscal 
balance by IDR 6trn, after accounting for 
oil-related revenues. This implies that for 
every $10pb increase, the deficit could 
be impacted by ~IDR 60trn, not far from 
MOF’s sensitivity of IDR 68trn (~0.25 -
0.30% of GDP) due to higher subsidy and 
compensation costs  (ICT assumed at 
$75bl vs prevailing Brent at $100bl). 
Spending will need to reprioritised to 
accommodate additional allocation 
towards subsidies and compensation.

                                                                                                                                            4

Next, the direction of travel on the 
contribution of SOE dividends from the 
investment agency to the state coffers 
for 2027 remains unclear, after being 
accounted for this year. Lastly, 
successive stimulus measures in-kind 
and wage subsidies were announced this 
year and could marginally add to the 
strain in 2027 (0.1-0.2% of GDP).

Discussions around the State Finance 
Law also warrant a watch. We maintain 
our deficit forecast at a wider -2.8% to -
2.9% range to account for any revenue 
shortfall or a gradual pace of growth. 
Nonetheless, deficits/debt are likely to 
stay below key thresholds  and not 
emerge as a flashpoint for the markets.

MONETARY POLICY ON AN EVEN 
KEEL

Bank Indonesia has left the benchmark 
rate unchanged  since July 2026, after 
100bp hikes in 1H26. While tighter global 
financial conditions and geopolitical 
risks were key risks on the horizon, the BI 
held the domestic GDP forecast at 4.9 -
5.7% (DBSf: 5.3%) alongside 
manageable inflation, notwithstanding 
El Nino-related risks. Inflation rose to 3.3% 
yoy in Sep26, towards the high end of the 
target range. Authorities have, however, 
assured that the impact of higher food 
prices due to poor weather will be 
addressed through administrative 
measures by regional bodies.

Contained inflation expectations ha ve 
reduced the urgency for BI to tighten 
policy further, allowing policymakers to 
prioritise growth and financial stability 
considerations. The emphasis on 
strengthening the balance of payments 
position reflects continued caution over 
external financing conditions and capital 
flows. However, with the rupiah 
expected to remain stable on the back of 
policy measures and fundamenta ls, the 
BI did not view current external 
pressures as severe enough to warrant 
additional tightening.

BI introduced a host of measures at the 
June to September policy meetings , 
including FX monetary hedging 
instruments and a mechanism to provide 
foreign investors with greater flexibility 
in managing IDR-denominated 
investments in Indonesia.  
These included –  
a) swap hedging per tenor bucket was 
increased to 3M - 15%; 6M - 20%; 12M - 
25%, from 12.5% earlier.  
b) DNDF incentive was increased to 6M - 
25% and 12M – 30%. An increase in FX/ 
NDF swap premium incentives for longer 
tenors will lower hedging costs and

### Page 5

Indonesia: Policy mix aligns October 12, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            5

encourage longer tenor positions, in turn 
increasing longevity of these flows;  
c) Launch of the VASTRA scheme (FX 
Market Transactions for Hedging 
through Partner Banks) got underway, 
with the naming of 14 domestic partner 
banks. This facility is designed to help 
investors transact with lower underlying 
documentation requirements, tap into 
longer banking hours , and access 
onshore hedging facilities.  
d) deepen FX and money markets by 
expanding Local Currency Transactions 
(LCT) between IDR and CNY , besides a 
few other currencies.

Policymakers are likely to tap a 
combination of administrative and 
macroprudential measures rather than 
solely interest rates to defend the 
currency as well as keep inflationary 
expectations in check.

We expect the pause on rates to extend 
into 4Q26.  This call might be at risk if 
depreciation pressures on the rupiah 
mount again on unfavourable global 
developments and if the US Fed 
undertakes a more aggressive hik ing 
cycle than our baseline (of 75bp hikes).

WIDER CAD WILL BE PARTLY 
OFFSET BY POSITIVE FLOWS

Indonesia’s 1H26 current account 
deficit (CAD) widened significantly  to -
$16bn vs -$3bn in the comparable period 
a year ago. Much of this deterioration 
was led by a narrower goods trade 
balance, while  the services deficit 
widened on the seasonal travel account 
due to the Hajj period. Higher coupon 
payments weighed on the primary 
account.  This pressure is likely to persist 
in 2H as the oil & gas trade deficit 
remains wide and export performance 
has been fi rm but tracking lower than 
regional peers, likely also impacted by 
the El Nino phenomenon in key agri 
commodities. We expect full year CAD to 
be closer to 1.8-2% of GDP from -0.1% in 
2025.

Much rests on the capital flows account. 
While 1H balance of payments (BOP) also 
widened, there was palpable 
improvement in 2Q26 vs 1Q.  2Q BOP 
narrowed significantly to $0.9bn from a 
wide $9.1bn in 1Q, helped by positive 
capital account flows – primarily 
stronger portfolio investment on SRBI 
and debt inflows, and net FDI position. 
This dependency is likely to extend to 2H, 
with capital fl ows till Sep26 holding up 
on account of hedging discounts and 
moves to improve inflows. Global 
conditions, including the narrow ID -US 
yields, however, warrant attention. 
Nonetheless, full-year BOP could remain

### Page 6

Indonesia: Policy mix aligns October 12, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            6

wide, on a scale comparable to the 2025 
gap.

IN SUMMARY

IDR markets have been relatively 
steady in October despite higher oil 
prices and a bid dollar , though 
sentiment remains sensitive to external 
developments and swings in global risk 
appetite. USDIDR traded within 17600 -
18000, while 10Y yield hovered around 
7.15-7.25%. A jump in the Aug goods 
trade surplus aided this respite, 
alongside foreign purchases of domestic 
debt, which have remained positive this 
month and on a  YTD basis. Insurers, the 
central bank, and retail investors have 
remained key anchors for the bond 
market. SRBI rates are off highs to sub -
7% levels but are attracting steady 
demand, likely encouraged by hedging 
discounts. An improved outlook for flows 
has helped contain spillovers from a 
wider current account deficit. This does 
not take away from the fact that the 
rupiah is amongst the worst performers 
on a year-to-date basis.

Separately, markets will also monitor 
developments in the political economy, 
institutional architecture, and formation, 
as well as the role of state -led agencies 
(e.g., DSI) and progress on the 
centralisation of decision -making. We 
expect the BI to keep on hold, however, 
pressure to tighten policy might return if 
the US Fed hikes rates well into 2027 
and the US dollar benefits from a risk-off 
environment, weighing on the currency.

## Document 343: Threats to European diesel imports_ risk of shortages ruled out for the time being, but increased risk of price rises (2026-10-01)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Threats to European diesel imports_ risk of shortages ruled out for the time being, but increased risk of price rises (2026-10-01).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 1st October 2026

EcoFlash

Threats to European diesel imports: risk of shortages ruled out for the time being, but increased 
risk of price rises

In Europe, the possibility of a US ban on diesel exports has heightened concerns about price trends for this widely used 
fuel1, which has already risen significantly since the outbreak of the conflict in Iran. It has become a major political issue 
on both sides of the Atlantic in the run-up to decisive elections.

A particularly tight diesel market due to a shortage of buffer stocks 
Following the outbreak of the conflict, diesel prices did indeed see some of the sharpest rises on the oil market due to particularly 
tight market conditions. Since the end of February, whilst the price of a barrel of crude has risen by 46%, diesel prices have soared 
by 100% in Europe and by 76% in the United States.

The factors alleviating pressure on the refined products market, and more specifically the diesel market, were more limited than in 
the case of crude oil. Shipments passing through the Strait of Hormuz (around 17% of maritime diesel trade prior to the conf lict) 
have fallen by around 75%. Added to this is the halt in Russian exports due to war damage, which is depriving the diesel market of 
around 12% of its maritime exports. Furthermore, unlike with crude oil, China has not acted as a stabilising  force in the diesel 
market. On the contrary, the energy crisis has led to a halt in Chinese exports of refined products (including diesel) to the  rest of 
Asia from April onwards. The resumption of Chinese exports over the last two months remains fragile, given, in particular, that 
diesel stocks are at their lowest level in 15 months.

The United States has eased these tensions in the diesel market by increasing its exports of refined products (up 25% on average 
in the first half of 2026 compared with the average over the last five years). This rise in exports has required high refinery utilisation 
rate (close to maximum capacity) and, to a lesser extent, a reduction in stocks (-11% compared with the average over the last five 
years as at mid -September for middle distillates 2). All these factors have contributed to tightening the US domestic market and 
pushed retail diesel prices to record levels. Road diesel prices (US average) have risen by 68% since the end of February and by 
74% year-on-year.

A structurally high level of vulnerability in Europe… 
Against this market backdrop, characterised by mounting tensions and very limited room for manoeuvre , Europe would be 
particularly hard hit by a reduction in – or even a complete halt to – US exports. According to Eurostat, imports accounted for 46% 
of European demand for diesel in the first half of 2026, with 40% of these imports coming from the United States. Europe, which

1 Diesel accounts for 17% of the primary energy mix in the EU and 7.3% in the United States. 
2 A category of refined petroleum products comprising mainly diesel and aviation fuel.

### Page 2

has lost a great deal of production capacity in recent years, is operating its refineries at near -maximum capacity. Its room for 
manoeuvre is therefore limited.

… But the risk of a shortage has been ruled out in the short term 
The risk of a shortage, however, appears limited in the short term. The situation regarding European stocks is not comfortable, but 
it is not alarming. Commercial diesel stocks in the ARA region 3 are currently at historically low levels (around 12 million barrels), 
23% below their level at the end of September 2025. As for emergency stocks (EU Emergency Stocks), these are, according to the 
European Commission, at a sufficient level. Eurostat estim ates this figure at 270 million barrels in June 2026 (stable from a year 
earlier), representing approximately 60 days’ worth of EU consumption. Europe imports around 0.9 mb/d of diesel from the United 
States, and emergency stocks would therefore cover around 60 days’ consumption at 4.5 mb/d.

The rise in prices could be significant 
Whatever the outcome of the conflict, diesel prices are likely to continue to rise in the short term. According to our base -case 
scenario, crude oil prices are expected to remain very high in Q4 2026 due to ongoing tensions in the Persian Gulf, whilst a return 
to normal levels of diesel exports from Russia and the Middle East appears unlikely by that time.

Among the factors likely to cause future tensions, the start of the maintenance season in Europe could affect production leve ls, 
whilst demand for diesel in the United States is typically high in the autumn (agricultural harvests and pre -winter storage). I n a 
scenario where tensions remain low, the diesel refining margin (crack spread) could rise by 5% in Q4 compared with its level at the 
end of September 2026, before gradually falling back during 2027. An intermediate scenario (a 10% rise in the crack spread in Q4 
2026) would result in moderate constraints on US diesel exports and a drawdown of European stocks. Finally, an adverse scenario 
(a 35% rise in the crack spread in Q4 2026) would imply severe restrictions on US diesel exports and a delayed European response.

What impact will this have on inflation in the euro area? 
According to our estimates, the rise in diesel prices would exacerbate the expected peak in inflation in the euro area without altering 
its timing. In our baseline scenario for September 2026 (see our recently published EcoPerspective), headline inflation would peak 
at 4.1% y/y in January 2027 before gradually falling back towards 2% by the end of the year. The two least adverse scenarios 
would add only a limited amount to the peak: +0.1 pp in the first scenario (+5% for the crack spread in Q4 compared with the end 
of Q3) and +0.2 pp in the intermediate scenario (+10%), with inflation reaching 4.2% and 4.3% respectively in January 2027. 
However, there is an upside risk in the second scenario linked to the uncertain extent of the spillov er effects. In both cases, the 
main impact would be felt through fuel prices (which quickly reflect rising market prices) and would fade over the course of 2027. 
In the case of the adverse scenario (+35%), the additional inflation would be close to 0.6 pp by early 2027, pushing inflation above 
4.6%. A shock of this magnitude would, in fact, reduce businesses’ ability to absorb rising costs, which would lead to price increases 
across a broader range of goods (food, industrial goods). Subsequently, the effect would only fade gradually.

Pascal Devaux 
Economist, Middle East, Western Balkans – Energy 
pascal.devaux@bnpparibas.com

Guillaume Derrien 
Economist, Europe, Eurozone, International Trade 
guillaume.a.derrien@bnpparibas.com

3 Amsterdam-Rotterdam-Antwerp, Europe’s main hub for the storage of raw materials.

仍在》
9月份紐元表現主要受外部因素主導 。儘管紐西蘭經濟
數據顯示改善跡象，但油價上升及美國財政赤字憂慮推
動美國長天期公債殖利率升至逾二十年高位，帶動美元
走強及市場重新評估美國利率前景 ，令NZD/USD由月
初接近0.59水平回落至0.56附近，紐元與其他高貝他貨
幣同步受壓。
經濟基本面方面 ，紐西蘭第二季 GDP季增0.2%、年增
2.6%，優於央行預期 ，顯示經濟持續復甦 。企業投資

及AI相關無形資產支出保持穩健，惟家庭消費及零售活
動仍然偏弱，反映內需復甦步伐相對溫和。貨幣政策方
面，市場預期RBNZ將於10月維持官方現金利率不變，
以評估既有緊縮政策的影響；惟若經濟活動及通膨壓力
持續展現韌性，年底前不排除進一步收緊政策的可能。
在主要已開發國家央行政策路徑逐漸分化的背景下，紐
西蘭相對較高的利率水準仍可為紐元提供一定支持，但
短期市場焦點預料仍集中於美國利率及美元走勢。
展望未來一至兩季，我們認為紐元仍有望受惠於國內經
濟成長改善及投資活動回暖，逐步擺脫近期低位。然而，
在美國利率維持較高水準及美元強勢延續的環境下，紐
元上行空間可能受到一定抑制 。因此，我們雖仍預期
NZD/USD 可自目前水準溫和回升，但將第四季目標價
由0.60下調至0.59，以反映美元強勢環境可能維持較長
時間。

通膨數據回落，6月降息的可能性加大重點摘要 《投資關鍵-政策觀望與美元強勢夾擊 ，加幣延續弱

中東局勢未見緩和，油價及通膨壓力維持高檔，促使
聯準會於9月16日升息25個基點，市場焦點亦由降息
預期轉向利差優勢及避險需求，導致非美貨幣承壓。
在市場風險情緒急轉直下，加幣自 USD/CAD 1.385
附近持續走弱，並一度來到 1.42以上。
加拿大央行料維持審慎觀望立場，市場對其轉向鷹派
的預期仍缺乏數據支持。儘管油價上升可能推升整體
通膨，但剔除能源因素後，加拿大經濟基本面尚未出
現足以支持進一步收緊政策的明顯改善。加上近期市
場已大幅回補加元空頭部位，在下一份就業報告公布
前，預料難有促使加拿大央行改變政策基調的催化劑，
因此貨幣政策前景仍偏向中性。
另一方面，高油價有助提升加拿大能源出口收入，對
加幣形成一定支撐；惟中東地緣壓力和美加貿易不確
定性猶存，料將拖慢加拿大經濟及就業復甦步伐。綜
合上述因素，我們預期加幣短期走勢仍偏疲弱，故將
4Q26美元兌加幣目標價由1.37上調至1.39。

《投資關鍵 –人民幣迎結匯需求與中美利差擴大的拉鋸》
人民幣與美元指數過去維持相關性，惟2025年下半年開
啟人民幣中期獨立升值行情。儘管兩者走勢高度相似，
但斜率拉開差距，人民幣升值動能顯著強於貶值方向。
今年以來中國景氣K型化樣態持續，出口景氣能見度高
於內需，累積大量貿易順差所引導結匯需求支持人民幣。
惟留意中國內需疲軟依舊，川習會落幕後，中國官方政
策焦點轉向穩增長與五中全會。除房市救市政策積極推
出外，中國人行宣布下調PSL 利率一碼至1.5%。中期來
看人民幣升值動能或受兩因素制約，首先中國經濟週期
挑戰加深不排除有資金流出壓力。其次，由於市場對美
國聯準會的升息預期加強，與中國寬鬆貨幣政策有所差
異，美中利差恐不利人民幣表態。
總結來看，4Q26人民幣將在結匯需求 、中美利差擴大

等因素拉鋸 ， 預 計 人 民 幣 走 勢 偏 向 溫 和 升 值 ，
USD/CNY Q4 6.65， 明年Q1 6.55。

《投資關鍵 -南非幣短線承壓，利差優勢支撐中期前
景》
南非幣近期承壓 ，美元兌南非幣由月初約 16.1升至
16.7水準，主要受美債殖利率攀升及美元轉強拖累 。
市場重新定價美國高利率將維持更久 ，帶動新興市
場資產普遍承壓 。此外，南非債市外資流出及避險
需求升溫 ，進一步加劇南非幣波動 ，使其表現落後
多數主要新興市場貨幣。
儘管外部環境轉趨逆風 ，南非國內經濟基本面仍展
現一定韌性 。近期零售銷售及消費數據表現穩健 ，
支持經濟溫和復甦 。雖然經常帳赤字 、外部需求疲
弱及油價上升、聖嬰現象（El Niño）帶來的糧食供
給風險 ，可能增加未來通膨壓力 ，但亦提高南非央
行維持偏鷹立場的必要性 。我們認為 ，在內需仍具
韌性及通膨風險升溫下 ，南非央行短期不急於轉向
寬鬆，較高利率環境可望持續提供南非幣利差支撐 。
短期而言 ，南非幣仍將受到美國利率 、美元走勢及
全球風險情緒主導 。若美債殖利率維持高檔 ，南非
幣可能持續面臨波動與資金流出壓力 。然而，相較
其他新興市場貨幣 ，我們對南非幣中期前景略趨正

面。一方面，南非央行偏鷹立場有助維持利差優勢；
另一方面 ，黃金價格回穩 、新興市場外匯波動率處
於相對低位 ，以及穩定的套息交易需求 ，均有利南
非幣表現。
重點摘要

## Document 431: シンガポール通貨庁､市場予想に反して金融政策を据え置き ～インフレ長期化と景気減速のリスクが併存、物価安定と景気配慮の両立が課題に～

Source PDF: all report pdf/シンガポール通貨庁､市場予想に反して金融政策を据え置き ～インフレ長期化と景気減速のリスクが併存、物価安定と景気配慮の両立が課題に～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-14
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年10 月 14 日

1/3 
シンガポール通貨庁､市場予想に反して金融政策を据え置き 
～インフレ長期化と景気減速のリスクが併存、物価安定と景気配慮の両立が課題に～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ シンガポール通貨庁（ＭＡＳ）は10 月 14 日、３会合連続の金融引き締めを見込む市場予想
に反し、金融政策を据え置いた。同国では、エネルギー・食料品価格の上昇により総合・コ
アインフレ率が加速する一方、旺盛なＡＩ・半導体関連需要が景気を支えている。 
○ ＭＡＳは、今後数四半期の景気は堅調に推移するものの、その後はＡＩ関連需要の減速や世
界的な金融環境の引き締まりにより成長が鈍化すると予想する。 また、 コアインフレ率は2027
年半ばから明確に低下すると見込み、2026 年の見通しを1.5～2.0％へと上限を引き下げた。
現行政策の下で名目実効為替レートが上昇を続けることで、輸入インフレを抑制できると判
断している。 
○ ただし、中東情勢の混乱や異常気象、肥料価格の高止まりにより、エネルギー・食料品を輸
入に依存する同国では、供給要因によるインフレが長期化するリスクがある。雇用環境にも
ばらつきがみられるなか、今回の据え置きは、既存の通貨高誘導による物価抑制効果を見極
めつつ、 景気下振れリスクにも配慮した判断と考えられる。 今後、 インフレが長期化すれば、
物価安定と景気への配慮を両立させる政策運営は一段と難しくなる可能性がある。

シンガポール通貨庁（ＭＡＳ）は、10 月 14 日、定例の金融政策見直しで、現行の金融政策を維
持すると発表した。ＭＡＳは、金融政策の調整手段として、名目実効為替レート（ＮＥＥＲ）の政
策バンド（許容変動幅） の幅、中央値、実勢上昇率（傾き） を用いる。４月の政策見直しでは、「実
勢上昇率を『わずかに』引き上げる」として、３年半ぶりの金融引き締めに舵を切った。さらに、
７月の前回見直しにおいても、「実勢上昇率を『ごくわずかに』引き上げる」と２会合連続の引き締
めに動いた。

### Page 2

2/3 
背景には、イラン情勢の悪化を受けた原油・天然ガス価格の上昇がある。同国は、一次エネルギ
ーの約 99％を石油・ 天然ガスに依存しており、 これらの価格上昇は幅広くエネルギーインフレを招
くことが懸念される。 一方で、 世界的に
ＡＩ （人工知能） ・ 半導体関連需要が旺盛
な動きをみせており、 関連財を多く輸出
する同国経済にとって景気の追い風と
なる展開が続いている。こうしたなか、
電気 ・ ガス料金や食料品価格などの上昇
を受けて、 総合インフレ率に加え、 住居
費と民間道路輸送費を除いたコアイン
フレ率も加速している （図１） 。このた
め、 事前の市場予想ではＭＡＳが３会合
連続の引き締めに動くとの見方が広が
っていたものの、予想外の据え置きを決定した。 
ＭＡＳは、会合後に公表した声明文で「経済は今後数四半期堅調なペースで拡大を続ける」もの
の、「その後はＡＩサイクルの例外的なペースが減速し、世界的な金融環境が引き締まることも重
なり成長の鈍化が見込まれる」 との見通しを示した。また、「コアインフレ率は、 供給要因による輸
入インフレが鈍化し、 国内のコスト上昇
圧力が抑制され、2027 年半ばから目に見
える形で緩和が見込まれる」 との見通し
を示した。2026 年のコアインフレ率の見
通しを 「1.5～2.0％」 と従来見通し （1.5
～2.5％）から上限を下方修正した。政策
運営については「現行の政策バンドによ
り、ＮＥＥＲは引き続き上昇し、短期的
に見込まれる輸入インフレの加速に歯
止めをかけることが可能」 、 「これを受け
て 2027 年のインフレ圧力の緩やかな後
退が確保できる」との認識を示した。金融市場では、根強い米ドル高圧力を背景にアジア新興国通
貨に下押し圧力がかかるなか、 足元のＮＥＥＲは緩やかに上昇しており （図２） 、 ＭＡＳはこの動き
がインフレ圧力を緩和すると判断したと考えられる。 
とはいえ、 イラン情勢は見通しが立たない状況が続いているうえ、 足元では混乱が中東全体に広
がりをみせており、原油・天然ガス価格の高止まりが長期化する 可能性は高まっている。さらに、
エルニーニョ現象の発生を受けて、 アジアでは異常気象が見込まれるなか、 肥料価格の高止まりも
重なり、農業生産の低迷が食料インフレを招く懸念も高まっている。同国は、エネルギーのみなら
ず、食料品も輸入に依存しており、供給要因によるインフレが長期化するリスクは高まっている。

### Page 3

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。
3/3
一方、同国では雇用全体の拡大が続くものの、人員削減の増加や求人の減少もみられるなど、雇用
環境にはばらつきが生じている。 今回の据え置きは、 既存の通貨高誘導による物価抑制効果を見極
めつつ、 世界的な金融環境の引き締まりやＡＩ・半導体関連需要の減速による景気下振れリスクに
も配慮した判断と考えられる。ただし、供給制約に伴うインフレが長期化すれば、物価安定と景気
への配慮を両立させる政策運営は一段と難しくなる可能性には注意が必要である。
以 上

## Document 436: トルコ株の混乱が映す「リラ建て資産誘導」の副作用 ～投資信託の急拡大で歪みが蓄積、市場への信認低下が新たなリスクに～

Source PDF: all report pdf/トルコ株の混乱が映す「リラ建て資産誘導」の副作用 ～投資信託の急拡大で歪みが蓄積、市場への信認低下が新たなリスクに～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-09-24
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年 9 月 24 日

1/3 
トルコ株の混乱が映す「リラ建て資産誘導」の副作用 
～投資信託の急拡大で歪みが蓄積、市場への信認低下が新たなリスクに～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ トルコ株式市場では、投資信託による低流動性銘柄への集中投資を背景に混乱が広がってい
る。政府はシムシェキ財務相の下、為替保護型預金（ＫＫＭ）を廃止し、国内貯蓄を株式や
投資信託などリラ建て資産に誘導してきた。一方、浮動株比率の低い企業の上場や投資信託
の急拡大により、特定銘柄への資金集中と株価上昇がさらなる資金流入を呼ぶ構造が形成さ
れ、流動性や価格形成の歪みが蓄積した。こうした問題を受けて資本市場委員会（ＳＰＫ）
が保有規制を強化すると、ポジション解消に伴う売りが広がり、投資信託の解約増加と市場
全体の株価下落につながった。 
○ 中銀は流動性供給を拡大し、ＳＰＫも一部投資信託の取引停止・清算や信用取引規制の一時
緩和などの対応に動いた。当局は問題を一時的かつ管理可能としているものの、今回の混乱
はトルコ株式市場が抱える集中リスクや取引慣行上の構造問題を浮き彫りにした。市場への
信認低下が長期化すれば、家計資金が外貨や金へ流出し、リラ相場や外貨準備への圧力が強
まるほか、ノンバンクを通じた住宅・自動車向け資金供給の縮小など実体経済に波及する可
能性もある。そうなれば、シムシェキ財務相が主導する経済政策への批判が強まる可能性が
あり、今後の市場動向を注視する必要がある。

トルコの株式市場が混乱している （図
１）。きっかけのひとつは、 指数算出会社
である MSCI が６月、 一部の投資信託によ
る株式保有の集中や、流動性の低い銘柄
に関して警告を発したことにある。その
結果、資産運用会社２社が浮動株の少な
い銘柄に投資する複数の投資信託につい
て、投資家による解約要請に応じられな
いと発表する事態に発展した。背景には、
2023 年に財務相 に返り咲いたシムシェ

### Page 2

2/3
キ氏の下、 同国政府は国内貯蓄をリラ建て資産にとどめる取り組みを進めてきたことがある。 具体
的には、2021 年に導入された為替保護型預金 （ＫＫＭ：リラ安による為替下落率が預金金利を上回
った場合、その差額を政府・中銀が補填する制度）を廃止し、株式や投資信託をはじめとするリラ
建て資産に誘導した。
一方で、 過去に政府は企業部門に対して、 借入ではなく株式市場を通じた資金調達を推奨してき
た。 その結果、 投資資金が株式市場に流入するなかで浮動株比率が低い企業が数多く上場したこと
で、 流動性や価格形成が歪められる構造的な問題が生じたとされる。 その後の投資信託の急拡大の
動きを受けて、投資家にとっては株式投資への新たな手段が創出される一方、 リスクの集中を招く
ことにつながった。 具体的には、 投資信託と関係の深い比較的流動性が低い銘柄への資金流入が活
発化し、 それに伴って株価が上昇して運用収益が向上し、 さらに新たな資金流入を呼び込むといっ
た循環が生まれた。こうした集中リスクや価格形成の歪み、市場操作の懸念に対応するため、資本

市場委員会 （ＳＰＫ）は８月、投資信託に対する保有ポジションの上限を厳格化し、2026 年末まで
に超過分のポジションの解消を義務付ける規制変更を行った。 具体的には、10 月末までに超過ポジ
ションの少なくとも３分の１、11 月末までに３分の２削減し、12 月末までに完全に解消するよう
求めた。その結果、株価を押し上げてきた動きが逆流し、株価下落により投資信託の解約請求が膨
らむ一方、 流動性の低い保有資産の売却が困難になったことを受けて、 現金確保を目的に流動性の
高い銘柄の売却を迫られ、市場全体に売り圧力が広がった。
こうした事態を受けて、中銀は９月 17 日、金融市場における流動性確保のため、レポ資金の供
給額を 3,000 億リラに引き上げるとともに、銀行間資金市場における銀行の借入限度額を10 倍に
引き上げた。さらに、ＳＰＫも同日、解約不履行を宣言した２社を含む、資産運用会社７社の投資
信託取引を停止するとともに、清算を命じた。なお、清算対象となる投資信託の資産総額は8,910

億リラにのぼるほか、投資家の数も 35 万人を上回るとの報道もある。また、ＳＰＫは市場支援を
目的に、 信用取引の最低証拠金維持率を10 月２日まで暫定的に35％から20％に引き下げた。金融
安定委員会（ＦＩＫ）は、一連の問題について、特定の投資信託に集中しており、一時的かつ管理
可能な性質のものとの見方を示すとともに、 より広範な構造的リスクに発展する可能性を否定して
いる。とはいえ、一連の混乱は、トルコ株式市場における集中リスクのほか、取引慣行に関する長
年の懸案事項が表面化したものと考えられる。
過去には、 トルコでは2022 年から翌23 年にかけても同様の動きがみられた。 投資家による株式
市場に対する信認低下が長期化すれば、 家計部門の貯蓄が再び外貨や金に流出していく可能性があ
り、 リラ相場や外貨準備への新たな圧力となることが懸念される（図２）。 さらに、 消費者向け融資

景気は弱い動き
中国景気は弱い動きが続いている。9月の製造業PMIは50.1と前月から上昇し、好不況の境目である50を3カ月ぶりに上
回った。構成指数をみると、生産は大きく上昇した一方、就業人員の低下が全体の改善ペースを抑制した。非製造業PMI

率は前年比＋4.3%と伸びが鈍化し、季節調整済み前期比（年率換算）も＋3.6%と政府目標の「4.5～5%」を下回った。

9月の米中首脳会談は一定の成果も、中心は対話継続の確認
9月24日、米国・ワシントンで米中首脳会談が実施された。5月の会談で打ち出された「建設的戦略安定関係」の構築を再
確認したほか、年内あと2回の会談の可能性が示唆されるなど、首脳間の対話の継続が確認された（下図）。
具体的には、米中双方が300億ドル相当の品目で関税引き下げに合意したほか、AI分野で対話枠組みを構築する方針で
合意するなど、個別分野では一定の進展が得られた。もっとも、総じてみると内容は概ね想定内であり、米中対立の全面
的な解消には至っていない。貿易戦争の休戦も今年11月までとされた期限が2カ月延長されたにとどまっており、今後も
両者間で対話を継続することを確認したことが最大の成果だったといえよう。

上昇品目
輸出価格は上昇も、デフレ輸出への懸念はなお強い
中国の輸出価格が足元で上昇している（左図）。2023年以降、EVや太陽光パネル、鉄鋼を中心に輸出価格は下落傾向
にあり、廉価品の輸出が海外市場を席巻する「デフレ輸出」が国際問題となったが、2026年4月以降、状況が一変した。品
目別でみると、世界的に需給が逼迫している集積回路やPC、PC部品の価格上昇が顕著である。
もっとも、足元の価格上昇は一部の品目に偏っている。HSコード4桁で分類した全1,204品目のうち、足元でも5割近くで価
格が下落している（右図）。このため、デフレ輸出への懸念は依然として根強い。

輸出価格 輸出価格が前年から上昇／下落した品目の割合
（出所） 海関総署 （注） 前年から横ばい品目は下落品目の上にグレーで表示（毎月0.0 ～0.7% の割合）（出所） 海関総署

## Document 506: 凱基投顧_NKE Nike_劉昃恩_20261005

Source PDF: all report pdf/凱基投顧_NKE Nike_劉昃恩_20261005.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-05
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

十月 05, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新  零售 ‧美國

Nike 
(NKE.US/NKE US)

每況愈下  
重要訊息 
Nike 1Q FY2 7 (財年止於 5 月；6-8 月) 獲利優於預期，主要來自費用裁 減。
受制於大中華區庫存去化及營運調整須再耗時多個季度，管 理層預計 FY27
營收年減高個位數，澆熄投資人之復甦期待 。 
評論及分析  
1Q FY2 7 財報尚可，係仰賴 費用縮減。Nike 1Q FY2 7 獲利 7.1 億美元，或
每股盈餘 0.48 美元，略優於預期，主因費用控管嚴格。營收年減 4%、但
季增 2%至 112 億美元，低於市場預估，反映 Nike 運動休閒系列、Jordan
品牌及大中華區表現疲軟。受惠供應鏈成本管理措施與匯率順風 (部分抵銷
自折扣增加及通路組合不利) ，毛利率年增 0.6 個百分點至 42.8%；再加上
營業費用抑制，營利率年持平於 7.9%，優於市場共識之 7.1%。 
大中華區展望低迷。 考量消費者購買謹慎與客流量不 足，管理層預計 FY27
營收年減高個位數， 隱含 2Q-4Q FY27 衰退擴大，再次不如市場預期。地區
別方面，大中華區尤為壓力核心 －預計庫存清理將再 耗時多個季度， FY27
業績惡化，且恐延伸至 FY28。短期而言，2Q FY27 北美及歐非中東的 促銷
活動亦較去年同期增加，影響營收約 4 個百分點，不利毛利率展望。  
庫存調整擴大至 Jordan 品牌。1Q FY2 7 庫存週轉天數年增 4%、但季減
11%至 108 天，反映公司正積極清理過剩庫存中，對比過去十年歷史平均
值為 101 天。繼 AJ1 及 Dunk 供應減量後，管理層現計畫進一步調降 Jordan
特定復古鞋款發售頻率，力求恢復供應稀缺模式；短期北美將受最大衝擊。
Jordan 品牌貢獻 1Q FY27 全球營收比重 13%，年減約中雙位數。  
投資建議  
Nike 組織調整與大中華區庫存去化時程較預期更長，且 消費者反饋黯淡導致
供應減量甚而擴及 旗艦 Jordan 品牌復古鞋款， 透露著欲振乏力跡象。 我們
維持 Nike「持有」評等，目標價調 降至 33.2 美元，係基於 FY27 每股盈餘
預估之 22 倍，對比歷史本益比區間為 18-55 倍。 
投資風險  
上檔風險為消費較預期強勁與品牌重拾人氣 。

美國 Nike

十月 05, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R   2

Nike 10月 2日法說會重點摘要 
 Nike 1Q FY2 7 稅後淨利 7.1 億美元，折合每股盈餘 0.48 美元，略優於
預期，主因費用控管嚴格。營收年減 4%、但季增 2%至 112 億美元，
低於市場預估 1%，反映 Nike 運動休閒系列  (營收佔比近半，年減低
雙位數)、Jordan 品牌及大中華區 表現疲軟。受惠供應鏈成本管理措施
與匯率順風 (部分抵銷自折扣增加及通路組合不利) ，毛利率年增 0.6 個
百分點至 42.8%；加上營業費用縮減，營利率年持平於 7.9%，優於
市場共識之 7.1%。 
 品牌別營收：Nike ( 佔總營收 98%)、 Converse (2%) 分別年減 4%、
28%。 
 市場別營收：北美 (佔總營收 46%) 年增 2%；其中，批發銷售年增
9%，抵銷直接銷售之 年減 6%。歐非中東 (28%) 與大中華區 (11%) 
銷售連兩季轉差 ，分別年減 5%、22%，係面臨促銷活動增加與 庫存
去化挑戰。亞太及拉美 (13%) 亦陷入年減 2%。 
 產品別營收：鞋類 (佔總營收 62%) 年減擴大至 6%，主因： (1) Dunk
業務本季削減近 50%，影響營收約 2 億美元；及 (2) 一些量大舊鞋款
銷量遜於預期。服飾  (30%) 年增 2%，配件 (5%) 則年減 3%。跑鞋 
(延續雙位數成長) 、Air Force 1  (健康成長)、足球 (年增雙位數)、訓練 
(歐非中東表現佳)、北美籃球  (年增雙位數) 及新款刷毛衣為少數亮點。  
 通路別營收：批發、直接 銷售分別年減 1%、8%。 
 1Q FY2 7 庫存週轉天數年增 4%、但季減 11%至 108 天，反映公司正
積極清理過剩庫存中，對比過去十年歷史平均為 101 天。管理層計畫
減少 Jordan 特定復古鞋款的發售頻率，力求恢復供應稀缺模式 ；短期
北美將受最大衝擊。 Jordan 品牌貢獻 1Q FY27 全球營收比重 13%，
年減約中雙位數。  
 考量消費者購買謹慎與 客流量尚顯不足，管理層預計 FY27 營收年減高
個位數；其中，大中華區尤為壓力核心， 預計庫存清理將再 耗時多個
季度， FY27 剩餘時間營收表現將 惡化，且恐延伸至 FY28。公司現估
FY27 稀釋後每股盈餘 1.15-1.35 美元 (尚未計入 Pace 重組計劃將帶來
約 0.15 美元之影響)，遠低於市場預期。  
 短期而言，管理層強調 2Q FY27 北美及歐非中東促銷活動較去年同期
增加，影響營收約 4 個百分點，不利毛利率展望。 
 Pace 組織調整計畫： 將業務層級簡化為三大區域，包含 ：美洲 (北美+
南美)、歐非中東，及亞洲 (大中華區+亞太平洋)，同時消除重工問題。
預計實施支出約 10 億美元，但將創造成本 節省約 25 億美元，大部分
效益將在 FY29-30 實現。

圖 6: 公司概況  圖 7: 營收來源地區多元 
Nike 成立於 1964 年，為全球最大運動用品製造及品牌商，市
佔率約 10-12%。旗下品牌包括 Nike、Air Jordan 及 Converse
等，主要透過直營店面及電商平台直接銷售或代理批發予合作
夥伴。鞋類產品貢獻營收大宗，佔比達 64%，其次為服飾的
9%及其他配件 7%。北美為最大的營收來源市場， FY26 營收
佔比達 44%，歐洲、中東及非洲為 27%，亞太平洋及拉美地
區 14%，大中華區 13%及其他 3%。

美國 Nike

十月 05, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R   6

項目 定義 
能源使用 
直接與間接能源消耗總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司運營範圍內消耗之能源總量 
- 能源使用總量 = 直接能源消耗總量 + 間接能源消耗量 
- 總能源使用量包括購買的能源、生產的能源 
- 就公用事業而言，輸電/電網損耗為其業務活動之一部分，被視為總能源消耗，數據不包括為滿足能源使用而生產的電力（公用
事業為出售而生產） 
- 就公用事業而言，用於能源生產的煤、天然氣或核能等原料不屬於「總能源使用」項下 
購買之再生能源 
Primary再生能源購買總量(十億焦耳) 
- 公司各種來源之能源消耗量與購買的能源中屬於自然界可再生者（太陽能、風能、水能、生物質能、地熱能）之量  
- 如無證據顯示再生能源由公司生產，則所報告的能源數據視為購買的再生能源 
再生能源使用率 再生能源占總能源消耗量之比例 
CO2 約當排放量 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量(公噸) 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 
(NF3) 
CO2 約當排放量營收比 
直接 CO2 與 CO2 約當排放量（公噸）銷售（百萬元）比 
- 公司擁有或控制的來源的直接排放量（範圍 1 排放量） 
- 相關氣體：二氧化碳 (CO2)、甲烷 (CH4)、一氧化二氮 (N2O)、氫氟碳化物 (HFCS)、全氟化合物 (PFCS)、六氟化硫 (SF6)、三氟化氮 
(NF3) 
廢棄物總量 
廢棄物總量(公噸) 
- 總廢棄物 = 無害廢棄物 + 有害廢棄物 
- 僅考慮固體廢棄物，但如液體廢棄物以公噸為單位呈報，則會將其加入求得包含液體廢棄物之總量 
- 對於採礦、石油與天然氣等行業，tailings、廢石、煤、飛灰等廢棄物亦考慮在內 
廢棄物回收率 
公司呈報的廢棄物回收率 
- 廢棄物回收率 = 廢棄物回收量/總廢棄物*100 
- 廢棄物轉化為能源或經由廢棄物焚燒產生能源視為廢棄物回收 
- 經由堆肥回收的廢棄物視為回收之廢棄物 
總取水量 
總取水量(立方公尺) 
- 由呈報組織直接或經水公司等中介機構從任何水源抽取的總水量 
- 水井、城鎮/公用事業/市政用水、河水、地表水等不同之水源均予以考慮 
環保支出 環保支出總金額 - 所有用於環境保護，或防止、減少、控制環境因素、影響、危害的投資與支出，亦包括處置、處理、衛生、清理支出 
員工流動率 
員工流動率 
- 包括任何原因（自願或非自願）離開公司的員工，如辭職、退休、自然離職/死亡、醫療失能、冗員、裁員、重組、解僱、裁減
或定期合約到期 
- 員工流動率 =（離開之員工/平均員工人數）*100 
- 平均員工人數 =（本年末員工人數 + 去年末員工人數）/2 
- 本財年末員工數 =上個財年末員工數 + 新員工數 - 離開之員工數 
女性經理 
女性經理百分比 
- 女性經理占公司經理的百分比 
- 如有不同階層之百分比，如最高階、高階、中階、初階，則會考慮中階女性經理之百分比 
- 女性經理百分比 = 女性經理人數/經理總數*100 
女性員工 
女性員工百分比 
- 女性員工占公司員工總數的百分比 
- 女性員工百分比 = 女性人數/員工總數*100 
教育訓練總時數 
所有員工教育訓練總時數 
- 僅考慮員工教育訓練時數 
- 包括一般員工所有類型的教育訓練（如健康與安全、環境、急難事件救援、技能與職業發展） 
- 如果數據以天為單位，則乘以 8，係假設 1 天 =  8 小時工作 
每位員工教育訓練時數 平均每年每位員工總教育訓練時數

股價說明 等級 定義 
 增加持股  (OP) 對個股持正面看法，預期個股未來十二個月的表現超越凱基證券集團所追蹤的相關市場的總報
酬。 
 持有 (N) 對個股持中性看法，預期個股未來十二個月的表現符合凱基證券集團所追蹤的相關市場的總報
酬。 
 降低持股 (U) 對個股持負面看法，預期個股未來 十二個月的表現低於凱基證券集團所追蹤的相關市場的總報
酬。 
 未評等 (NR) 凱基證券未對該個股加以評等。  
 受法規限制 
未評等 (R) 
受凱基證券集團內部政策和 /或相關法令限制使凱基證券集團無法進行某些形式的資訊交流，其
中包括提供評等給投資人參考。  
  *總報酬 = (十二個月目標價 -現價)/現價

## Document 520: 十五年来最大单周净买入！Citadel分析师：散户又开始就买AI股了

Source PDF: all report pdf/十五年来最大单周净买入！Citadel分析师：散户又开始就买AI股了.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-14
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
散⼾抄底 AI 股，⼀周净买⼊ 91 亿美元创 15 年纪录！ Citadel Securities 数据显⽰，资⾦⾼度
集中于半导体与 AI 基础设施，但⾮⼴泛追涨，⽽是⽅向性重仓，买⼊偏斜达过去⼀年第 90
百分位。
摘要：
散⼾投资者正以罕⻅的⼒度重返⼈⼯智能交易。
Citadel Securities 股票与衍⽣品策略主管 Scott Rubner 在最新研报中指出，上周散⼾投资者通过
该公司平台净买⼊美国股票 91 亿美元，创下近 15 年来最⼤单周净买⼊纪录，资⾦⾼度集中于 AI
相关股票及 ETF 。
这⼀资⾦回流推动纳斯达克 100 指数与标普 500 指数双双刷新历史⾼点，尽管市场对 AI 泡沫的警
告声从未消散。对于关注科技板块的投资者⽽⾔，散⼾的⼤规模回归正成为⽀撑当前涨势的重

要⼒量。
半导体与 AI 基础设施成资⾦主要流向
上周散⼾买盘的结构⾼度集中。据 Scott Rubner 披露，散⼾净买⼊半导体股票约 26 亿美元，为
过去⼀年中第三⾼的单周买⼊规模，并使 10 ⽉有望成为该平台上芯⽚股净买⼊规模最⼤的⽉
份。与此同时， AI 基础设施相关股票也录得约 24 亿美元的净买⼊。
值得注意的是，上述资⾦涌⼊发⽣在芯⽚股承压、市场对 AI 景⽓持续性存有疑虑的背景之下，显
⽰散⼾对 AI 主线的信念并未因短期波动⽽动摇。
⽅向性押注增强，⽽⾮交易量扩张
Rubner 在研报中强调，此轮散⼾买⼊规模的扩⼤，主要源于 " 更强的⽅向性确信度，⽽⾮整体交
易量的抬升 " 。数据显⽰，散⼾上周买⼊量较卖出量⾼出 2.7% ，对应过去⼀年周度⽅向性偏斜的
第 90 百分位⽔平。与此同时，整体交易活跃度仍处于过去⼀年的第 42 百分位，低于平均⽔平。
这⼀组合意味着，散⼾并⾮在⼴泛追涨，⽽是在主动、集中地加注 AI ⽅向，信号意义相对明
确。
Rubner ⽉初预判已获验证

此次散⼾回流并⾮突发。 Rubner 在 10 ⽉初便已准确预判了这⼀趋势，彼时他指出，经历 9 ⽉的
明显降温后， 10 ⽉为投资者提供了更佳的 " 补仓 " 窗⼝，即将到来的财报季与企业回购潮将为市场
提供⽀撑。
据彭博此前报道， Rubner 当时明确表⽰，散⼾现⾦正在 " 重新装填 " ，等待重返美股。上周的数
据印证了这⼀判断，也令市场对后续散⼾资⾦能否持续⼊场保持⾼度关注。

2－（2）米国共和党政権の特徴
 戦後の共和党政権は、軍人出身のアイゼンハワーを例外に、 すべての政権が戦争遂行

## Document 548: 大摩：存储股在周期顶部往往看似最便宜，但也最危险

Source PDF: all report pdf/大摩：存储股在周期顶部往往看似最便宜，但也最危险.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

摩根⼠丹利指出，三星和 SK 海⼒⼠⽬前远期市盈率仅约 3 ⾄ 4 倍，看似便宜，却可能暗藏周
期顶部的估值陷阱，即便 AI 存储超级周期仍在延续。历史上，存储股往往领先盈利峰值⻅
顶： 2008 年和 2012 年提前约 4 个⽉， 2018 年提前约 8 个⽉。随着新增产能于 2027 年下半年
逐步释放，供需变化可能引发新⼀轮周期拐点。
摘要：
AI 驱动的存储芯⽚超级周期仍在延续，但存储股的投资逻辑正在发⽣变化。
摩根⼠丹利最新报告指出，尽管供需紧张、价格上涨和盈利改善仍为⾏业提供强劲⽀撑，但随
着股价⼤幅上涨、盈利预期集中上修，存储股的乐观预期已得到充分定价，进⼀步上涨的空间
⾯临考验。
核⼼⽭盾在于，存储⾏业基本⾯越强，周期顶部的⻛险反⽽越容易被低估。  历史上，存储股往
往在盈利达到峰值之前⻅顶，因为市场习惯将当期的⾼盈利⽔平外推⾄未来，导致股票在⾏业
景⽓度最⾼时，估值看起来反⽽最便宜。
在此背景下，⼤摩认为，存储⾏业的⾼景⽓未必能转化为存储股的持续超额回报。随着市场对
盈利增⻓的预期不断抬⾼， AI 算⼒与⽹络供应链的相对投资吸引⼒正逐渐超过存储板块。

超级周期延续，但周期性并未消失
本轮存储⾏业的繁荣有坚实的需求基础。
AI 数据中⼼建设持续推⾼内存需求，⾼带宽内存（ HBM ）占 AI 服务器芯⽚成本的近半。⻓期供
货协议通过锁定采购承诺、设置价格底线等⽅式，提⾼了供应商的收⼊可⻅性。与此同时，
HBM ⽣产挤占传统 DRAM 产能，新⼀代 AI 模型对内存容量的需求不断提升，进⼀步加剧供需紧
张。
⼤摩数据显⽰，当前 HBM 每 GB 价格已达到历史峰值的近 7 倍，反映出本轮存储周期的强劲程
度。但⾏业增⻓的结构性增强，并不意味着存储股的投资回报也会随之改善。 ⾼利润终将吸引
新增产能，随着供给追上需求，或下游进⼊库存消化阶段，价格和盈利就可能触顶。 2000 年、
2016 年、 2019 年和 2022 年的历史周期均体现了这⼀规律。
报告认为，本轮周期的拐点可能更晚出现，但仍需警惕 2027 年以后供需格局的变化。
盈利预期过⾼，低市盈率暗藏陷阱
本轮周期⾃ 2025 年 9 ⽉启动以来，亚洲存储股累计上涨约 9 倍，总市值已迈⼊万亿美元级别。⼤

摩数据显⽰，市场对亚洲存储公司的每股收益（ EPS ）⼀致预期平均上调了 432% ，反映出市场
对本轮周期的盈利前景抱有极⾼期待。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⽣活家 搜索

### Page 2

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
然⽽，盈利持续增⻓不代表股价仍有同等幅度的上涨空间。三星和 SK 海⼒⼠⽬前的远期市盈率
约为 3 ⾄ 4 倍，表⾯上估值便宜，却也可能是周期股在盈利⾼峰附近出现的估值陷阱。⼤摩指
出，若要⽀撑当前股价隐含的进⼀步上涨空间， DRAM 合同价格还需在现有⽔平上环⽐上涨约
70% ，实现难度很⼤。
历史经验同样值得警惕： 2008 年和 2012 年，存储股均⽐盈利峰值提前约 4 个⽉⻅顶； 2018 年，
这⼀时间差扩⼤⾄ 8 个⽉。存储股往往在盈利最强、估值看似最便宜时，率先反映市场对未来景
⽓回落的担忧。
HBM 与 DRAM 景⽓延续，供给⻛险渐近
短期来看，存储⾏业基本⾯仍有⽀撑。⼤摩预计， HBM 价格将在 2027 年继续上涨，传统 DRAM
和 NAND 合同价格也有望在 2026 年第四季度进⼀步⾛⾼。 AI 服务器需求旺盛、传统 DRAM 产能

向 HBM 转移，以及企业级固态硬盘（ eSSD ）需求增⻓，共同⽀撑当前的涨价⾏情。
但供给端的变化不容忽视。存储⼚商正⼤幅增加资本⽀出，新增产能预计从 2027 年下半年开始
逐步释放。与此同时，消费级 NAND 市场可能⾯临价格回落压⼒，不同存储品类的景⽓表现或将
出现分化。
数据中⼼架构演进也可能改变产业链的收益分配。⼤摩测算，英伟达 NVL72 单机架系统中存储
相关内容占⽐约为 29% ，扩展⾄ 8 个互联机架后降⾄ 19% ，⽽⽹络设备占⽐则从 10% 升⾄ 20% 。
随着 AI 集群规模扩⼤，⽹络硬件在系统价值中的权重提⾼，存储⼚商未必能同⽐例受益于基础
设施投资增⻓。

南亞科 (2408 TT)

DRAM 明年仍緊缺

目標價上調至 660 元，潛在上漲空間+25% 
1. 3Q26 營收 825.49 億元，QoQ+68%；其中價漲料逾 6 成，優於富邦預期。 
2. 產業供不應求將持續，2027 年毛利率料逾 8 成。 
3. 市場正逐步拉高 DRAM 明年仍供不應求之預期，預料南亞科評價可望上調。

3Q26 價漲料逾 6 成，單季 EPS 估 26.22 元 
南亞科累積 7-9 月合併營收 1336.49 億元，QoQ+61.9%，YoY+611.7%，數字優
於富邦預期。由於公司銷售量大致 QoQ 持平，絕大部分季成長來自售價上揚；因
此我們預估本季毛利率約 87.0%，較前季上揚 7.5 個百分點。簡言之，我們預估南
亞科 3Q26 營業利益估約 1,102 億元，稅前淨利估 1,117 億元，歸屬母公司稅後淨
利估約 905 億元，EPS 估約 26.22 元。

DRAM 明年仍緊缺，價格修正料將延後 
市調機構及美光法說不約而同指出，DRAM 產業 2027 年供不應求將較今年更加嚴
峻，可見未來尚無法看到供需平衡的時間點。其次，南亞科 7 月法說已指出當時已
簽定之長短期 LTA 供貨合約佔總產能 50%，且下游客戶已依照 LTA 基礎價格低標
提供一定的預付款 。 無獨有偶 ，美光 10 月 1 日(台北時間)指出已簽署 26 份 SCA(策
略客戶協議)，目前協議涵蓋了約 2030 年以前約 35%的營收。簡言之，我們目前已
將 DRAM 價格修正的時點延後到 4Q27 或更晚。

產業供不應求將持續，27 年毛利率料逾 8 成 
展望 4Q26，我們預期 Consumer DRAM 漲幅約+3~8%、其他傳統 DRAM 漲幅
約 10~15%；4Q26EPS 估約 25.11 元，全年EPS 約 73.43 元。至於 2027 年，Fab 
5A(泰林新廠)預計 2H 開始有意義挹注產出；由於 DDR4 升級到 DDR5 的情況擴
大，銷售組合預料逐漸往 DDR5 放大，因此每 GB 平均售價可能因此出現 QoQ 下
滑。我們預估南亞科合併營收約 6,143 億元，YoY+56.2%；其中位元銷售量年增估
約二成 、ASP 年增約估三成 。簡言之 ，富邦預估南亞科 2027 年毛利率約達 86.3%，
營業利益估約 5,050 億元 ， 歸屬母公司稅後淨利估約4,163 億元 ，EPS 估約 120.66
元。

富邦不同之處 
富邦修正南亞科最新之 2026-27 年 EPS 預估值分別為 73.43、120.66 元，相較目
前 CMoney 同業平均預估值分別+10%、+12%，數字相對樂觀。

評價與風險 
我們預料美光 10 月 1 日法說後 ， 一部分投資人將逐步認同 DRAM 明年仍將供不應
求，售價也不會出現修正，南亞科股價預料將逐步墊高。簡言之，我們維持南亞科
買進的投資評等，目標價上調至 660 元(約 FY26 的 9.0PER 或 FY26 4.6XPBR)。南
亞科的股價風險來自(1)高階伺服器產品或私募股東合作進度不如預期、(2)AI 產業
展望出現意外的結構修正。

## Document 626: 沙特加速布局霍尔木兹绕行方案，东西管道扩容计划浮出水面

Source PDF: all report pdf/沙特加速布局霍尔木兹绕行方案，东西管道扩容计划浮出水面.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-14
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

⾯对霍尔⽊兹海峡封闭及袭击⻛险，沙特计划在两⾄三年内将东西管道运⼒提升 43% ⾄
1000 万桶 / ⽇，同时探索多元化出⼝路线与海外储油，以构建更具韧性的能源供应链。
摘要：
沙特阿拉伯正全⼒推进⽯油出⼝基础设施的战略重组。在霍尔⽊兹海峡封闭的背景下，沙特能
源⼤⾂ Prince Abdulaziz bin Salman 宣布，计划在两⾄三年内将东西管道输送能⼒从⽬前的 700
万桶 / ⽇⼤幅提升⾄ 1000 万桶 / ⽇，增幅达 43% 。
这⼀扩容计划在利雅得举⾏的 WPC 能源⼤会上公布。该管道在霍尔⽊兹海峡封闭后已成为沙特
原油出⼝的核⼼通道，⽬前每⽇通过红海港⼝延布转运约 400 万桶原油。然⽽，这条战略要道并
⾮⽆懈可击 ——9 ⽉ 11 ⽇，沙特将其归咎于伊拉克⺠兵武装的⽆⼈机袭击，迫使管道被迫关闭。
管道中断对市场供应造成直接冲击。能源⼤⾂表⽰，截⾄ 10 ⽉ 6 ⽇，管道流量已恢复⾄ 580 万桶 /
⽇，但仍较当前 700 万桶 / ⽇的最⼤设计容量低出 120 万桶。与此同时，沙特阿美⾸席执⾏官 Amin

Nasser 在同⼀场合表⽰，公司正研究增设原油出⼝路线，并扩⼤海外储油能⼒。
管道成为霍尔⽊兹封闭后的出⼝⽣命线
这条全⻓ 740 英⾥、⼜称 " ⽯油⼲线 " （ Petroline ）的管道，连接沙特东部油⽥与红海沿岸的延布
港，在霍尔⽊兹海峡封闭后迅速从辅助通道升级为主⼲出⼝路线。据 Zawya 报道，沙特已借助该
管道将约 400 万桶 / ⽇的原油绕开霍尔⽊兹，改道经延布港出⼝。
此前，有媒体曾追踪记录到该管道单⽇流量⼀度触及 700 万桶 / ⽇的历史峰值。此次宣布的扩容⽬
标，意味着沙特将在现有基础上再增加 300 万桶 / ⽇的输送能⼒，届时单条陆上管道的运⼒将超越
全球多数主要产油国的⽇产量。
替代路线同样⾯临安全威胁
绕⾏霍尔⽊兹的⽅案并⾮⾼枕⽆忧。原油经延布港出港后，仍需通过曼德海峡（ Bab el-Mandeb
Strait ）才能抵达亚洲主要市场，⽽这⼀咽喉要道⾯临胡塞武装的威慑。据报道，伊朗⽀持的也
⻔胡塞武装今年 7 ⽉已公开宣称将阻断沙特经该海峡的油轮通⾏。

这意味着，即便东西管道扩容计划如期落地，沙特出⼝供应链在红海段仍将⾯临持续的地缘政
治⻛险。对于依赖中东原油供应的亚洲买家⽽⾔，替代航线的安全性将是评估供应稳定性的重
要变量。
沙特强调能源韧性，阿美着眼多元布局
在 WPC 能源⼤会上， Prince Abdulaziz bin Salman 对沙特的能源韧性作出系统性阐述。他表
⽰，能源韧性 " 不仅要通过维护、修复、改道、替代和恢复能⼒来衡量，更要通过在压⼒下持续
供应的能⼒来检验 " 。
Amin Nasser 则在战略层⾯进⼀步补充，沙特阿美正与能源部协调，研究新增原油出⼝路线，同
时扩⼤海外战略储油布局。他还表⽰，公司正运⽤前沿技术强化设施检测，并加速受损设施的
修复进程。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⽣活家 搜索

### Page 2

## Document 640: 电脑卖不动，但联想们更赚钱了

Source PDF: all report pdf/电脑卖不动，但联想们更赚钱了.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

利润和供应链话语权都在重新分配。有的公司赚得更多了，有的从电脑之外获得了更多收
⼊。
摘要：
电脑更难卖了。第三季度，全球 PC 出货量减少了两成。
IDC 最新公布的数据显⽰， 2026 年第三季度，全球 PC 出货量为 6270 万台，同⽐下降 20.1% 。联
想、惠普、戴尔、苹果、华硕，排名前五的⼚商⽆⼀增⻓。连⼀向坚挺的苹果， Mac 出货量也跌
了 11.3% 。
按理说，这样的数据出来，⾏业应该⼀⽚哀嚎。但翻开⼏家⼚商截⽌到 6 ⽉底或 7 ⽉底的财报，
业绩并不差。联想的经调整净利润涨了近两倍，苹果的 Mac 收⼊创了同期纪录，戴尔股价年内涨
了三倍。华硕则是在这轮暴跌⾥抢到了更多市场份额。只有惠普，收⼊创了新⾼，利润却在下
滑。
同样是内存涨价，结果却两样，原因是 AI 正在放⼤⼚商之间的差异。数据中⼼抢⾛存储产能，
内存价格暴涨，整机⼚跟着提价。有⾼端⽤⼾买单的，价格涨得上去；没有的，只能硬扛。与
此同时，科技巨头砸向 AI 的钱变成了服务器订单，流向⼿⾥有基础设施业务的 PC ⼚商。简⽽⾔

之，有的公司赚得更多了，有的从电脑之外获得了更多收⼊。
所以 PC 市场的失速，不只是需求不⾏了，⽽是 AI 重洗了这个⾏业的成本、利润和座次。我们想
弄清楚，这场暴跌中，为什么有的⼚商受伤，有的反⽽赚得更多？ AI ⼜在怎样改写这个⾏业的秩
序？
旺季为什么不灵了？
PC ⾏业有个规律。三季度有返校季，有企业集中采购，渠道还要为年末备货，历来是⼀年⾥的
出货⾼点。今年这个规律失灵了。三季度全球出货量同⽐暴跌 20.1% ，环⽐⼆季度跌了 8.1% 。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⽣活家 搜索

### Page 2

去年同期的换机潮，抬⾼了今年⽐较的基数。当时， Windows 10 临近停⽌常规⽀持，带动企业
和个⼈更新电脑。
今年上半年，⼚商和渠道⼜把⼀部分原计划下半年进的货提前了。 IDC 统计的出货包括发往渠道
和直接交付给⽤⼾的机器。电脑进⼊经销商仓库，就已经计⼊出货。
渠道为什么提前压货？因为怕涨价。今年年初，集邦咨询预测⼀季度内存合约价要环⽐涨五到
六成，后来的实际涨幅接近翻倍。现货市场更疯狂，⼀条 16GB 的 DDR5 内存条，去年 9 ⽉卖 300
块，今年 1 ⽉卖到 1400 块，四个⽉涨了三倍多。对准备在接下来⼏个⽉卖电脑的商家⽽⾔，等待
可能意味着更⾼的进货成本。
提前采购可以暂时避开后⾯的涨价，但也占⽤了资⾦。到了三季度，渠道需要先卖掉之前的库
存，新订单随之放慢。
⼀位华北地区的 PC 经销商告诉「定焦 One 」，上半年⼤家都在抢货，怕后⾯更贵，变相撑起了市
场，到了三季度，多数都在慢慢消化库存，不敢向⼚商下新订单。
⿇烦在于，市场判断，存储芯⽚的供需紧张⾄少要持续到 2028 年上半年。为什么会持续这么

久？还是因为 AI 。
PC 和 AI 服务器使⽤的内存不完全相同。普通 PC 主要使⽤ DDR 、 LPDDR 系统内存，采⽤⾼端
GPU 的 AI 服务器还需要⾼带宽内存，也就是 HBM 。但它们都需要三星、 SK 海⼒⼠、美光等⼚商
安排产能。
数据中⼼的订单，对存储⼚越来越有吸引⼒。⼤型云⼚商愿意接受更⾼的价格、签订⻓期供货
协议，存储⼚⾃然将产能往 AI 倾斜，留给 PC 的就少了。缺⼝短期补不上， PC ⼚商只能加价抢剩
下的货。⼀位 PC 渠道⼈⼠告诉「定焦 One 」，这⼀轮涨价，说⽩了就是算⼒太⽕， AI 抢⾛了上游
产能， PC 的内存价格只能往上⾛。
上游成本涨到⼀定程度，整机⼚也不得不提价，并直接影响了销量。 Omdia ⾸席分析师 Ben Yeh
估算，DRAM和固态硬盘在PC成本中的占⽐，已从通常约15%升⾄接近40%。
卖得少了，为什么赚得多了？
台数少卖了这么多，为什么有的⼚商还能赚得更多了？
⼀是提价，把内存成本转嫁给买家；⼆是靠 PC 之外的⽣意，尤其是 AI 服务器。

### Page 3

先看提价。提价之后能留下多少利润，各家公司差别很⼤。有的客⼾愿意继续购买，有的会推
迟换机；⼚商多收的钱，还要先⽀付更贵的零部件。⼀位 PC 渠道⼈⼠对「定焦 One 」表⽰，中
端价位现在最难受。
相对从容的是苹果和华硕，这两家的三季度出货量跌得最少。苹果跌 11.3% ，华硕跌 8.6% ，都
好于⾏业整体 20.1% 的降幅。
苹果的逻辑⼤家都不陌⽣，⾃研芯⽚，软硬件⽣态结合。 Mac 在截⾄ 6 ⽉的季度⾥收⼊ 103.5 亿
美元，涨了 29% 。 6 ⽉，苹果继续上调多款 Mac 和 iPad 的售价，其中 512GB 版 MacBook Air 从
1099 美元涨⾄ 1299 美元。
Omdia 在报告中认为，⾼端定位减轻了涨价对苹果的影响。对已经习惯 Mac 及其软件的⽤⼾，换
电脑时除了⽐较价格，还要考虑原有软件和使⽤习惯。
华硕⾛的是另⼀条路，底层逻辑相似。这家早年打性价⽐的⼚商，这些年硬⽣⽣切进了游戏本
和创作者市场。 ROG 和 ProArt 拼的是更强的图形性能、更适合创作的屏幕，或者持续运⾏⼤型

软件的能⼒。⽬标受众愿意为了性能多付⼏千块。华硕⼆季度品牌业务营业利益 196 亿新台币，
同⽐涨了 230% ，公司称⾼价值产品占系统业务收⼊的 65% 以上。华硕的利润增⻓，也有 AI 服务
器的贡献，⼆季度，其基础设施解决⽅案已占品牌收⼊的 36% 。不过，它还是主要靠⾼端 PC 赚
钱。
PC （ System ）是华硕最⼤单⼀业务  图源  / 华硕 2026Q2 财报
另⼀类是联想和戴尔，尽管它们的跌幅超过⾏业整体，分别达到 23% 和 25% ，但这两家并不
慌。它们的策略是 PC 省着花， AI 基建猛投钱。两家的基础设施业务都贡献了更多收⼊和利润。
先说戴尔。最新财季，总营收 470 亿美元，涨了 58% 。其中 AI 服务器收⼊ 164 亿美元，翻倍增
⻓，已经超过整个 PC 相关业务的收⼊。财报发布后，戴尔股价单⽇涨了近 16% ，市值冲上 3600
亿美元。
联想的基础设施业务也开始贡献更多利润。截⾄ 6 ⽉底的季度， ISG 业务（包括传统服务器、 AI
服务器和存储）收⼊同⽐增⻓ 98% ，营业利润达到 7.77 亿美元，上年同期则亏损 0.86 亿美元。 AI

服务器项⽬储备 540 亿美元，⼀个季度涨了 157% 。
最难的是惠普。三季度，惠普出货量暴跌 30.9% ，是前五家中降幅最⼤的，市场份额从⼀年前的
19.1% 缩⽔到 16.5% ，⼀个季度让出 2.6 个百分点。

### Page 4

惠普也涨价了。在截⾄ 7 ⽉底的那个季度，它少卖了 16% 的 PC ，个⼈系统业务收⼊反⽽涨了
18% ，冲到 118 亿美元。收⼊保住了，但利润没保住。提价的速度追不上内存涨价的速度，个⼈
系统的营业利润率被压到 4.6% ，低于公司的⻓期区间。
惠普没有苹果那样的⾼端定价权，主⼒产品是 500 到 800 美元的商务本和家⽤本，这个价位的⽤
⼾对价格更敏感。它也没有 AI 基建的牌， PC 贡献四分之三营收，但利润薄，六成利润仍来⾃打
印。打印业务本⾝也需求疲软。
惠普两头都不占，公司预计，下半年整个 PC 市场还要同⽐跌⼗⼏个百分点。
这五家的财报反映出两个趋势。⾸先是需求侧，利润正在向⾼端集中。Omdia 的数据提到，售价
900 美元以上的⾼端 PC ，出货量在这轮暴跌中没有下降，还略有增⻓。苹果和华硕吃的正是这
块市场。
其次是供给侧，⽐的是谁拿得到紧缺的货，谁涨价市场认。这背后说到底，是供应链议价能⼒
的差距。摩根⼠丹利的供应链调研给过⼀个排序，戴尔拿到的供货和价格条件最好，联想次

之，惠普因为更依赖消费市场，排在最后。
还有哪三张牌？
PC 这个⾏业，过去四⼗年都是薄利多销的⽣意，主要通过规模采购、制造效率和渠道销售控制
成本。即便是内存涨跌，也是周期性的，⼚商可以通过调节库存和订单节奏来消化成本。
这次不同。 PC ⼚商能暂时靠涨价保住利润，但出货量还会跌， IDC 已经把全年预期下调到跌
11.3% 。接下来撑不撑得住，要看⼚商怎样使⽤⼿⾥的三张牌。
⼀是找新货源。今年下半年，惠普、华硕和宏碁已开始在部分笔记本中少量使⽤⻓鑫内存。联
想则更早将⻓鑫的内存和⻓江存储的固态硬盘⽤在了部分在售机型上。
图源  / ⻓鑫存储官⽹
这在过去很难想象。 PC 内存多年来被三星、 SK 海⼒⼠、美光三家垄断，合计份额超过九成。惠
普、戴尔这样的美国品牌，供应链⾥此前基本没有中国⼚商的位置。现在存储芯⽚涨得太多、
供给紧张， PC ⼚商必须给⾃⼰找第⼆货源。
不过这张牌只能缓解，治不了本。⻓鑫报价与三星等主要供应商相当，由于需求旺盛， PC ⼚商
也难以提前锁定当季之后的货源，排队拿货的局⾯没有变。

### Page 5

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

写评论
第⼆张牌是更及时地调整售价。过去整机⼚和客⼾每个季度重新谈⼀次价，现在内存⼀周⼀个
价，重定价的窗⼝从 60 到 90 天压缩到 0 到 15 天。戴尔⾸席运营官 Jeff Clarke 在 5 ⽉业绩会上形
容，当时的调价频繁到 “ 感觉每天都在重新定价 ” ，⼤型订单则需要逐笔谈价。调价快⼀天，就少
垫⼀天的钱。
第三张牌是 AI PC 。主流⼚商基本都在押注，机构预计今年 AI PC 渗透率过半。但上述 PC 渠道⼈
⼠称，现在 AI PC 能做的事，普通 PC ⼤多也能做，只是慢⼀点，多数⽤⼾短期内不会为⼀个快
⼀点的功能换电脑。微软官⽅说明，普通 Windows 11 设备就能使⽤ Copilot 等云端 AI 体验，部分
本地功能才有专⻔的硬件要求。

上述 PC 渠道⼈⼠补充道，上⼀轮换机潮是微软停服带来的，下⼀轮则要有⾜够重要的 AI 应⽤。
它⼀旦出现，被涨价压住的消费需求会集中释放，那才是 PC ⾏业的下⼀波⾏情。
前两张牌能帮助电脑⼚商对冲⼀部分成本压⼒，但能不能留下更多利润，还要看供应商涨了多
少价，客⼾⼜愿意接受多少。过去⼀年，三星、 SK 海⼒⼠、美光三家的市值先后突破了万亿美
元，合计超过 4 万亿美元，做内存的成了这轮 AI ⾏情最⼤的赢家。⼀位半导体⾏业分析师告诉
「定焦 One 」： “PC 这个⾏业，利润⼤头从来不在整机⼚⼿⾥。过去三⼗年拿⼤头的是英特尔和
微软，整机⼚赚组装的⾟苦钱，现在轮到存储⼚了。”
利润往上游⾛的同时，下游也重排了座次。近⼀年，戴尔股价涨了 290% ，市值到了 3700 亿美
元。联想涨了 170% ，市值约 4200 亿港元。惠普的股价在财报发布时跌了 9% 。市场更看好戴尔
和联想这样有基础设施业务的⼚商，惠普还属于 “ 硬件制造 ” 。市值的差距就这么拉开了。
AI 改变了这个⾏业的利润分配。过去，利润 = 出货量 × （单价 - 成本），出货量是最⼤的变量。现

在， AI 提⾼了电脑⼚商的采购成本，也给部分公司增加了新的⽣意，增量利润流向有⾼端⽤⼾或
AI基建业务的⼚商。出货量下跌，只是这个旧公式失效的外在表现， PC ⾏业往后拼的是谁能守
住⾃⼰最赚钱的⽣意。有⾼端⽤⼾的，守住⾼端⽤⼾；有服务器订单的，把订单做成利润。
本⽂来源：定焦 One

## Document 724: 谷歌Gemini 4 Argon全球上线，推广价截至11月底，Pro用户可在搜索AI模式体验

Source PDF: all report pdf/谷歌Gemini 4 Argon全球上线，推广价截至11月底，Pro用户可在搜索AI模式体验.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-13
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

将近三周前⾕歌发布 Gemini 4 Argon 时仅⾯向⽹络安全合作伙伴，本周⼆上线后，普通⽤
⼾使⽤该模型需订阅 Google AI Pro 或 Ultra ，开发者通过付费版 Gemini API 接⼊， Google
AI Studio 和 Antigravity 向 Ultra 订阅⽤⼾开放。新模型输出上限提升⾄ 100 万 Token ，⾕歌
特别介绍其在机器⼈领域应⽤；推⼴价 2 美元 / 百万输⼊ Token 、 10 美元 / 百万输出 Token ，
仅为竞品 GPT-6 Astra 和 Claude Fable 5.1 的五分之⼀。
摘要：
⾕歌将旗舰 AI 模型 Gemini 4 Argon 推向全球⽤⼾。在此前近三周仅向⽹络安全防御合作伙伴开放
后，这款模型周⼆正式扩⼤开放范围，⾯向普通⽤⼾、开发者和企业提供服务。⾕歌希望借助
其在编程、复杂推理和⽹络安全领域的能⼒，进⼀步巩固在前沿 AI 竞争中的位置。
美东时间 10 ⽉ 13 ⽇周⼆，⾕歌宣布， Gemini 4 Argon 已在全球范围内上线。 Google AI Pro 和

Ultra 订阅⽤⼾可以在 Gemini 应⽤及⾕歌搜索的 AI 模式（ AI Mode ）中体验该模型；开发者和企
业⽤⼾可通过付费版 Gemini API 调⽤。 Ultra 订阅⽤⼾还可在 Google AI Studio 和 Antigravity 中使
⽤该模型，企业⽤⼾则可通过 Gemini 企业服务及 Antigravity 接⼊。
⾕歌 CEO Sundar Pichai 在社交媒体上宣布新模型正式上线。⾕歌 DeepMind 表⽰， Argon 旨在
从头到尾解决复杂问题，在编程、企业⼯作流和⽹络安全防御⽅⾯提供前沿能⼒。⾕歌 AI
Studio 产品负责⼈ Logan Kilpatrick 则称，该模型过去⼀个⽉已在⾕歌内部经过超过 10 万⼈的测
试，研发团队为此次发布投⼊了约六个⽉时间。
此次全球上线意味着⾕歌此前采取的谨慎发布策略进⼊新阶段。随着模型能⼒增强可能带来的
⽹络安全⻛险引发科技界⼴泛讨论，⾕歌选择先让专业防御者使⽤ Argon ，待相关机构有机会修
补漏洞、修复模型发现的系统缺陷后，再逐步扩⼤开放范围。

从⽹络安全 “ 定向开放 ” 到全球上线，⾕歌扩⼤模型使⽤范围
⾕歌于 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇⾸次发布 Gemini 4 Argon 时，并未⽴即向公众开放，⽽是通过 Fairwind 计划向经
过筛选的⽹络安全防御者提供访问权限。
⾕歌当时表⽰，⾯对前沿模型⽇益增强的⽹络安全能⼒，需要采取分阶段发布策略，让防御者
有时间识别和修补漏洞，同时持续完善安全防护机制。
此次扩⼤开放，标志着⾕歌开始将这款旗舰模型的能⼒推向更⼴泛的消费级和商业场景。
不过，开放并⾮没有⻔槛：普通⽤⼾需要订阅 Google AI Pro 或 Ultra ；开发者通过付费版 Gemini
API 接⼊； Google AI Studio 和 Antigravity 则向 Ultra 订阅⽤⼾开放。⾕歌还允许符合条件的科学
家和研究⼈员申请免费使⽤ Antigravity 最⾼⽤量档，进⼀步推动模型在科研领域的应⽤。
输出上限提升⾄ 100 万 Token ，瞄准复杂编程与⻓流程任务
Argon 的核⼼定位并⾮简单回答问题，⽽是处理需要⻓时间推理、多步骤执⾏的复杂任务。

⾕歌表⽰，新模型针对⼤型软件⼯程、企业知识⼯作和⽹络安全防御进⾏了优化，可⽤于⼤型
代码库迁移、复杂⼯程问题、法律与⾦融研究等任务。与传统的⼀问⼀答式交互相⽐，这类⼯
作往往要求模型在较⻓时间内保持上下⽂连贯性，并反复规划、执⾏和检查结果。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⽣活家 搜索

### Page 2

在技术规格⽅⾯， Argon 的⼀项重要突破是将最⼤输出 Token 上限从此前的 6.4 万提升⾄ 100 万。
这意味着，在适⽤的后台执⾏模式下，模型能够⽣成更⻓的连续输出，为⼤型代码修改、复杂
研究报告和多步骤智能体⼯作流提供更⼤空间。需要注意的是， 100 万 Token 是最⼤输出能⼒，
并不意味着每次调⽤都会⽣成如此⻓的内容。
⾕歌 AI Studio 产品负责⼈ Kilpatrick 称， Argon 在编程能⼒、知识储备和持续处理任务⽅⾯均取得
进展。⾕歌此前还将模型⽤于内部⼯程⼯作，最新公布的案例包括对⼤型代码库进⾏⾃动化重
构与优化，体现了其⾯向真实开发流程的定位。
除软件开发，⾕歌本周⼆还发布了 Argon 在机器⼈领域的应⽤介绍。
该模型能够结合多视⻆视频、三维⼏何信息、物理模拟及机器⼈⾃⾝状态数据，推理物体之间
的物理关系，并将视觉理解、代码⽣成与机器⼈控制衔接起来。⾕歌希望借此推进模型在真实
物理环境中的应⽤。
以下可⻅ Argon 控制着⼀台波⼠顿动⼒（ Boston Dynamics ）的  Spot 四⾜机器⼈学习⼀项新技

能：踩踏脚踏式垃圾桶以丢弃汽⽔罐。在实际踩踏踏板并将汽⽔罐投⼊开启的垃圾桶之前，
Argon 会先创建⼀个脚踏式垃圾桶的  MuJoCo 关节式数字孪⽣模型，以此来练习相关动作。
19 项基准测试中 13 项单项领先，正⾯对标 OpenAI 与 Anthropic
在 9 ⽉ 30 ⽇⾸次发布时，⾕歌公布了 Gemini 4 Argon 在 19 项基准测试中的表现。按照⾕歌当时披
露的成绩，最⾼思考档版本在其中 13 项测试中取得单项最⾼成绩，另有 1 项并列第⼀。
这些结果显⽰， Argon 尤其强调知识密集型任务、⻓上下⽂推理和复杂编程能⼒。其主要竞争对
⼿包括 OpenAI 的 GPT-6 Astra ，以及 Anthropic 的 Claude Fable 5.1 等前沿模型。相关成绩为⾕歌
重返全球顶尖模型竞争提供了依据，但基准测试领先并不意味着模型在所有任务和实际应⽤中
都优于竞争对⼿。
此次全球上线，也让这些能⼒从受限的安全测试环境进⼊更⼴泛的⽤⼾场景。对于⾕歌⽽⾔，
接下来的关键不仅是模型在基准测试中的表现，还包括开发者采⽤情况、复杂任务的实际完成

质量，以及模型能否在企业⼯作流中持续创造价值。
推⼴期 API 价格低于主要竞品， 11 ⽉底后价格翻倍
价格是 Argon 争夺开发者和企业客⼾的另⼀项重要筹码。

⾕歌为⾯向公众开放的 Argon 设置了限时推⼴价格，⾄ 2026 年 11 ⽉ 30 ⽇⽌，每百万输⼊ Token 收
费 2 美元，每百万输出 Token 收费 10 美元。推⼴期结束后，价格将分别升⾄ 4 美元和 20 美元，输
⼊、输出价格均翻倍。
缓存输⼊ Token 的价格折扣则达到 95% 。按照推⼴期的输⼊价格计算，命中缓存的输⼊ Token 成
本约为每百万 0.10 美元；调价后则约为每百万 0.20 美元。对于需要反复调⽤相同背景材料、处理
⻓上下⽂或执⾏多轮任务的应⽤⽽⾔，缓存机制有望降低部分使⽤成本。
按照此前披露的竞品标准 API 价格， GPT-6 Astra 和 Claude Fable 5.1 的输⼊、输出 Token 价格分

别为每百万 10 美元和 50 美元，均为 Argon 推⼴期价格的五倍。 Claude Opus 5.5 的对应价格为 4
美元和 20 美元，与 Argon 推⼴期结束后的定价相当。不同模型的计费规则、缓存政策和任务表现
存在差异，实际成本仍取决于具体⼯作负载。
总体来看，⾕歌此次发布释放出两项信号：⼀⽅⾯， Gemini 4 Argon 开始从⽹络安全领域的有
限测试⾛向更⼴泛的商业应⽤；另⼀⽅⾯，⾕歌正试图通过⻓上下⽂、复杂任务执⾏能⼒和有
竞争⼒的初期定价，吸引开发者将其纳⼊实际⼯作流程。模型能否进⼀步扩⼤市场份额，仍有
待后续的⽤⼾采⽤率和真实业务表现检验。
